# <span class="deva-large">आ</span> <span class="iast-large">ā</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">796 entrées</span>
  <a href="/" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--aa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰आ</span> <i>-ā</i> suffixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] utilisé après les verbes nominaux et thèmes verbaux secondaires pour désigner l'activité verbale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--aam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰आम्</span> <i>-ām</i> forme des parfaits péri. ind. à composer avec un verbe auxiliaire au parfait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--aana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰आन</span> <i>-āna</i> ifc. de <a href="#entry--maana" class="skt-link">-māna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--aanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰आनी</span> <i>-ānī</i> forme des noms f. not. d'épouses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आ</span> <i>ā</i> pf. à, vers (le locuteur) ⟨vb. de mouvement⟩ (sens réfléchi) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> un peu, tirant sur ⟨s. de couleur ou quantité⟩ (ex. <i>ānīla</i> bleuâtre, <a href="#entry-o_s_na" class="skt-link">oṣṇa</a> tiède) — prép. depuis ⟨abl.⟩; jusqu'à, et même jusqu'à, aussi loin que ⟨abl.⟩ — conj. de plus, et même <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> part. de réminiscence, de compassion ou de douleur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ā</i>.<br> <i>ca</i> <i>...</i> <i>ā</i> à la fois ... et.<br> <i>ā</i> <i>evam</i> en est-il donc ainsi?.<br> <i>ā</i> <i>samudrāt</i> jusqu'à la mer.<br> <i>ājanubāhu</i> a. m. n. f. dont les bras descendent jusqu'aux genoux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_dambara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आडम्बर</span> <i>āḍambara</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-_dambara" class="skt-link">ḍambara</a>] <a href="#entry-aa_dambara" class="skt-link">m.</a> tambour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tumulte, vacarme; comportement bruyant; emphase <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> barissement des éléphants; sonnerie de trompette pour l'attaque — <a href="#entry-aa_dambarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āḍambarī</i> ifc. immensité de; ultime, sublime; tonitruant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_dhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आढक</span> <i>āḍhaka</i> a. <a href="#entry-aa_dhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_dhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_dhakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āḍhakī</i> qualificatif de mesure de poids de grain, quart; un <a href="#entry-aa_dhaka" class="skt-link">āḍhaka</a> vaut 4 <a href="#entry-prastha" class="skt-link">prastha</a> (environ 3,5 kg) ou 4 <a href="#entry-pu_skala" class="skt-link">puṣkala</a> (256 poignées); il y en a 4 dans un <a href="#entry-dro_na" class="skt-link">droṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_dhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आढ्य</span> <i>āḍhya</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-arthya" class="skt-link">arthya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aa_dhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_dhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_dhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āḍhyā</i> riche; qui abonde en ⟨i.⟩.<br> <i>āḍhyaṃkaraṇo</i> <i>mantraḥ</i> formule magique pour devenir riche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आणव</span> <i>āṇava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-a_nu" class="skt-link">aṇu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aa_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_nava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aa_navaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṇavā</i> atomique; infinitésimal — <a href="#entry-aa_nava" class="skt-link">n.</a> extrême petitesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_navamala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āṇavamala</i> [<a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>] <a href="#entry-aa_navamala" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] souillure première de l'âme, qui empêche sa libération; sa nature est celle de l'ignorance [<a href="#entry-aj_naana" class="skt-link">ajñāna</a>]; elle réduit la conscience à une limite finie en occultant le Soi.<br> <a href="#entry-aa_nava" class="skt-link">āṇava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आणि</span> <i>āṇi</i> <a href="#entry-aa_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. broche d'essieu d'un char; cheville <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fémur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_saa_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आषाढ</span> <i>āṣāḍha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-a_saa_dha" class="skt-link">aṣāḍha</a>] <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. mois des <a href="#entry-Ua_saa_dhaas" class="skt-link">Aṣāḍhās</a> (juin-juillet); on le double certaines années pour que le calendrier lunaire rattrape le calendrier solaire; cf. <a href="#entry-manasaapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">manasāpañcamī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. bâton de bois d'arbre <a href="#entry-palaaza" class="skt-link">palāśa</a>, tenu par un ascète lors de rites effectués durant ce mois [<a href="#entry-puurvaa_saa_dhaa" class="skt-link">pūrvāṣāḍhā</a>] — <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṣāḍhī</i> astr. jour de sa pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>]; on y célèbre la fête de <a href="#entry-gurupuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">gurupūrṇimā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_topa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आटोप</span> <i>āṭopa</i> <a href="#entry-aa_topa" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>āṭopās</i> gonflement, redondance; orgueil, suffisance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आटु</span> <i>āṭu</i> <a href="#entry-aa_tu" class="skt-link">m.</a> arch. étage, not. du toit d'un temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabdika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आब्दिक</span> <i>ābdika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abda" class="skt-link">abda</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabdika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabdika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabdikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābdikā</i> annuel — ifc. -ennal; qui dure tant d'années.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभृ</span> <i>ābhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ābharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ābhṛta</i><i>)</i> apporter; approcher, amener; transporter avec soi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> remplir; attirer l'attention de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabh_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभृत</span> <i>ābhṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aabh_r" class="skt-link">ābhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabh_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhṛtā</i> apporté; amené; procuré; produit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rempli; plein.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभ</span> <i>ābha</i> [<a href="#entry-aabhaa_2" class="skt-link">ābhā_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhā</i> qui a l'aspect de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaa_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाणक</span> <i>ābhāṇaka</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-bhaa_naka" class="skt-link">bhāṇaka</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaa_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> dicton, proverbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaa_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाष्</span> <i>ābhāṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ābhāṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ābhāṣita</i><i>)</i> parler à, converser; appeler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाष</span> <i>ābhāṣa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_s" class="skt-link">ābhāṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> parole; adresse, commande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dicton, proverbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> introduction, préface.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaa_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाषण</span> <i>ābhāṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_s" class="skt-link">ābhāṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaa_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de s'adresser à, conversation avec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaa_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाषित</span> <i>ābhāṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_s" class="skt-link">ābhāṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaa_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaa_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhāṣitā</i> à qui l'on s'adresse; à qui l'on a parlé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभास</span> <i>ābhāsa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aabhaas_1" class="skt-link">ābhās_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> splendeur; aspect, apparence; émanation, manifestation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fantôme; illusion, apparence trompeuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. sophisme, paradoxe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [esthétique] prétension d'une émotion [<i>bhāvabhāsa</i>] ou d'une humeur [<i>rasabhāsa</i>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ābhāsana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> illumination; illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ābhāsatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaasataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'être une illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ābhāsatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaasatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être une illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ābhāsavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaasavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>] théorie ontologique de la réalité physique comme aspect ou illusion de la Réalité absolue.<br> <a href="#entry-aabhaasa" class="skt-link">ābhāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभासिन्</span> <i>ābhāsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aabhaas_1" class="skt-link">ābhās_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aabhaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhāsinī</i> qui a l'apparence de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaasvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभास्वर</span> <i>ābhāsvara</i> [<a href="#entry-aabhaas_1" class="skt-link">ābhās_1</a><a href="#entry--vara" class="skt-link">-vara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhaasvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaasvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaasvaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhāsvarā</i> qui brille — <a href="#entry-aabhaasvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. illusion, classe de divinités mineures, sous-classe du <a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>; il y a 64 <i>ābhāsvarās</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभात</span> <i>ābhāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_1" class="skt-link">ābhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhātā</i> qui brille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> visible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभाति</span> <i>ābhāti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aabhaa_1" class="skt-link">ābhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhaati" class="skt-link">f.</a> splendeur, lumière; ombre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभरण</span> <i>ābharaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«ce qui est porté»] ornement, costume, bijoux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhi_seka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभिषेक</span> <i>ābhiṣeka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abhi_seka" class="skt-link">abhiṣeka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhi_seka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhi_seka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhi_sekaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhiṣekā</i> relatif au sacre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhiik_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभीक्ष्ण</span> <i>ābhīkṣṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abhiik_s_na" class="skt-link">abhīkṣṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhiik_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhiik_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhiik_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhīkṣṇā</i> répété; fréquent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhiik_s_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभीक्ष्ण्य</span> <i>ābhīkṣṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abhiik_s_na" class="skt-link">abhīkṣṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aabhiik_s_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> répétition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. action répétitive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभीर</span> <i>ābhīra</i> <a href="#entry-aabhiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom d'un peuple d'éleveurs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vacher, éleveur de troupeau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. caste mixte issu du mariage d'un <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> et d'une femme de caste <a href="#entry-amba_s_tha" class="skt-link">ambaṣṭha</a> — <a href="#entry-aabhiirii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhīrī</i> femme de caste <a href="#entry-aabhiira" class="skt-link">ābhīra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> langue du peuple <a href="#entry-aabhiira" class="skt-link">ābhīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभिस्</span> <i>ābhis</i> [i. pl. <a href="#entry-iyam" class="skt-link">iyam</a>] ind. par ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhyaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभ्याम्</span> <i>ābhyām</i> [i. du. <a href="#entry-iyam" class="skt-link">iyam</a>] ind. par ces deux — abl. de ces deux — dat. à ces deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhyantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभ्यन्तर</span> <i>ābhyantara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abhyantara" class="skt-link">abhyantara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhyantara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhyantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhyantaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhyantarā</i> interne, situé à l'intérieur; opp. <a href="#entry-baahya" class="skt-link">bāhya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभ्यस्</span> <i>ābhyas</i> [abl. pl. <a href="#entry-iyam" class="skt-link">iyam</a>] ind. de ces — dat. à ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabhyudayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आभ्युदयिक</span> <i>ābhyudayika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-abhyudaya" class="skt-link">abhyudaya</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aabhyudayika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhyudayika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aabhyudayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ābhyudayikā</i> qui apporte la prospérité — <a href="#entry-aabhyudayika" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie de <a href="#entry-zraaddha" class="skt-link">śrāddha</a> effectuée à l'occasion d'un heureux événement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aabila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आबिल</span> <i>ābila</i> cf. <a href="#entry-aavila" class="skt-link">āvila</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आचार</span> <i>ācāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aacar" class="skt-link">ācar</a>] <a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> comportement, conduite, pratique; démarche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coutume, règle, rite; loi commune, usage, tradition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. bonne conduite; conduite comme critère de jugement moral; opp. <a href="#entry-vicaara" class="skt-link">vicāra</a> — a. <a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ācārā</i> ifc. dont la conduite est de nature ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>sādhvācāraḥ</i> m. <a href="#entry-saadhvaacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>sādhvācārā</i> qui se conduit bien.<br> <i>kalyāṇācāraḥ</i> qui se conduit bien.<br> <i>ācāraḥ</i> <i>paramo</i> <i>dharma</i> <i>ācāraḥ</i> <i>paramaṃ</i> <i>tapaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>ācaraḥ</i> <i>paramaṃ</i> <i>jñanamacārat</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>na</i> <i>sādhyate</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> La bonne conduite est le plus grand devoir, c'est la discipline première, c'est la connaissance suprême; que ne peut on accomplir par la bonne conduite?<br> <i>deśācārastāvat</i> <i>ādau</i> <i>vicintyaḥ</i> <i>deśe</i> <i>deśe</i> <i>yā</i> <i>sthitis</i> <i>saiva</i> <i>kārya</i> [<a href="#entry-Uvaraahamihira" class="skt-link">Varāhamihira</a>] En tout premier lieu, on doit observer la tradition locale; dans tous les pays c'est la coutume qu'il est bon de suivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaaraafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ācārāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aacaaraafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> jn. la discipline monastique, premier chapitre [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] du canon <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaarahiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ācārahīna</i> [<a href="#entry-hiina" class="skt-link">hīna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aacaarahiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaarahiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaravarjitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ācāravarjitā</i> qui ne suit pas les conventions <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. exclu; hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaaravarjita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ācāravarjita</i> [<a href="#entry-varjita" class="skt-link">varjita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aacaaravarjita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaravarjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaravarjitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ācāravarjitā</i> qui ne suit pas la coutume; irrégulier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. exclu; hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaaravat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ācāravat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aacaaravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaravat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aacaaravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ācāravatī</i> qui se conduit bien; vertueux — <a href="#entry-aacaaravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ācāravatī</i> lit. [DKC.] np. d'Ācāravatī «Vertueuse», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dagho_sa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍaghoṣa</a>; elle mourut en couches, donnant naissance à <a href="#entry-Uma_nikar_nikaa" class="skt-link">Maṇikarṇikā</a>, qu'elle avait promise comme épouse au fils de <a href="#entry-Ukaantimatii" class="skt-link">Kāntimatī</a> en perdant aux dés avec celle-ci.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आचार्य</span> <i>ācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. maître, précepteur spirituel; professeur [«qui règle la conduite»]; not. enseignant du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>; il investit l'étudiant avec le cordon sacré [<a href="#entry-yaj_nopaviita" class="skt-link">yajñopavīta</a>].
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ācamati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ācamanīya</i><i>)</i> boire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. se rincer la bouche pour se purifier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aacamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

</div>

</div>

</div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(ādatte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>ādāya_2</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> prendre, enlever (de ⟨dat.⟩); prendre avec soi, emporter; dérober; reprendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accepter; recevoir (de ⟨loc. i. abl.⟩).

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदर</span> <i>ādara</i> [<a href="#entry-aad_r" class="skt-link">ādṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aadara" class="skt-link">m.</a> considération, respect, attention, soin; affection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tenant compte de ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>ādareṇa</i> var. <i>ādarat</i> adr. respectueusement; avec soin, avec égard.<br> <i>snehādareṇa</i> adr. amicalement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadara_niiya">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadarza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>

</div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ādharṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ādhṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ādhṛṣya</i><i>)</i> attaquer, assaillir.
  </div>

</div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aadh_r_s" class="skt-link">ādhṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadh_r_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadh_r_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadh_r_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhṛṣyā</i> qui peut être attaqué; approchable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

[<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(ādadhāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āhita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ādheya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> placer sur, mettre, déposer — pr. md. <i>(ādhatte)</i> accepter, recevoir; garder.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधान</span> <i>ādhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aadhaa" class="skt-link">ādhā</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> depôt; fait de disposer (not. le feu rituel) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prise; réception; effort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. consécration des feux védiques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhaara">

[<a href="#entry-caandra" class="skt-link">cāndra</a>] locatif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] support de méditation; cf. <a href="#entry-_so_dazaadhaara" class="skt-link">ṣoḍaśādhāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] substrat.<br> <i>ādhārādheyabhāva</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] relation entre un objet et son réceptacle ou support.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādhāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] fait de servir de support ou de réceptacle.
  </div>
</div>
