[<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] capacité de servir de support ou de réceptacle; localité de résidence spatio-temporelle.<br> <a href="#entry-aadhaara" class="skt-link">ādhāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadham">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधम्</span> <i>ādham</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-dham" class="skt-link">dham</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ādhamate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ādhmāta</i><i>)</i> se gonfler, s'enfler; être plein.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधेय</span> <i>ādheya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aadhaa" class="skt-link">ādhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādheyā</i> à poser, à placer; à donner, à conférer; à attribuer, à concéder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce qui est reçu ou déposé — <a href="#entry-aadheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> placement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] attribut, prédicat — ifc. instauration de, installation de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadheyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādheyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-aadheyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. relation de prédication.<br> <a href="#entry-aadheya" class="skt-link">ādheya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhibhautika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधिभौतिक</span> <i>ādhibhautika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhibhuuta" class="skt-link">adhibhūta</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhibhautika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhibhautika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhibhautikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhibhautikī</i> phil. dérivé ou produit des êtres ou éléments naturels; matériel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séculier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhidaivika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधिदैविक</span> <i>ādhidaivika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhidaivika" class="skt-link">adhidaivika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhidaivika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhidaivika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhidaivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhidaivikā</i> phil. concernant ou provenant d'êtres surnaturels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhikya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधिक्य</span> <i>ādhikya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhika" class="skt-link">adhika</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhikya" class="skt-link">n.</a> surplus, excès; supériorité, prépondérance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhipatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधिपत्य</span> <i>ādhipatya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhipati" class="skt-link">adhipati</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhipatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> suprématie; souveraineté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhmaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आध्मात</span> <i>ādhmāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aadham" class="skt-link">ādham</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhmaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhmaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhmaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhmātā</i> gonflé, enflé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhunika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधुनिक</span> <i>ādhunika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhunaa" class="skt-link">adhunā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhunika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhunika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhunikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhunikā</i> neuf, nouveau, récent; actuel; moderne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधू</span> <i>ādhū</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-dhuu_1" class="skt-link">dhū_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(ādhunoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ādhūta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> secouer, agiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आधूत</span> <i>ādhūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aadhuu" class="skt-link">ādhū</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhūtā</i> secoué, agité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dérangé; qui tremble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhvarika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आध्वरिक</span> <i>ādhvarika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhvara" class="skt-link">adhvara</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhvarika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhvarika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhvarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhvarikā</i> soc. qui concerne le sacrifice de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> — <a href="#entry-aadhvarika" class="skt-link">m.</a> livre [<a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>] expliquant le sacrifice <a href="#entry-adhvara" class="skt-link">adhvara</a>; prêtre connaissant son rituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhvaryava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आध्वर्यव</span> <i>ādhvaryava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhvaryu" class="skt-link">adhvaryu</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhvaryava" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. service du prêtre sacrifiant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhyaatmika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आध्यात्मिक</span> <i>ādhyātmika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-adhyaatmika" class="skt-link">adhyātmika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādhyātmikī</i> qui concerne le soi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qui concerne l'âme suprême; de nature spirituelle — <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmika" class="skt-link">n.</a> maladie mentale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadhyaatmikataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādhyātmikatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmikataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> spiritualité.<br> <a href="#entry-aadhyaatmika" class="skt-link">ādhyātmika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदि</span> <i>ādi</i> <a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> conception, commencement; principe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> premier, primordial, suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. symb. le nombre 1 — iic. suprême — ifc. précédé de, commençant par; et ainsi de suite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. mot composé commençant par «⟨iic.⟩» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. racine appartenant à la classe de conjugaison [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] caractérisée par «⟨iic.⟩»; cf. <a href="#entry-bhvaadi" class="skt-link">bhvādi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>ādika</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>ādyau</i> les deux premiers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>ādayas</i> <i>et cætera</i>; les éléments commençant par ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. not. désigne une liste du <a href="#entry-ga_napaa_tha" class="skt-link">gaṇapāṭha</a> commençant par «⟨iic.⟩» — loc. <i>ādau</i> adv. à l'origine; en premier, tout d'abord.<br> <i>ādim</i> <i>ādatte</i> il fait un commencement.<br> <i>ādav</i> <i>eva</i> adv. juste avant; juste en ce moment.<br> <i>indrādayaḥ</i> <i>surāḥ</i> les dieux commençant par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> et les autres dieux.<br> <i>bhūvādayas</i> pl. gram. les racines verbales (qui commencent par <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a> dans le lexique [<a href="#entry-dhaatupaa_tha" class="skt-link">dhātupāṭha</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदिष्ट</span> <i>ādiṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aadiz" class="skt-link">ādiś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādiṣṭā</i> instruit, renseigné, informé; enjoint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> annoncé, indiqué, prescrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadibuddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādibuddha</i> [<a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a>] <a href="#entry-aadibuddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. d'Ādibuddha, le <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> primordial.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadigdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदिग्ध</span> <i>ādigdha</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-digdha" class="skt-link">digdha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadigdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadigdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadigdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādigdhā</i> frotté d'onguent; oint.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadiirgha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदीर्घ</span> <i>ādīrgha</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-diirgha" class="skt-link">dīrgha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadiirgha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadiirgha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadiirghaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādīrghā</i> allongé, ovale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadikaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādikāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-aadikaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> épith. du <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rāmāyaṇa</a>, le «Poème primordial».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadikavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādikavi</i> [<a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>] <a href="#entry-aadikavi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Ādikavi, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> le «Poète primordial».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādimatī</i> qui a un début.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadimattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādimattva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-aadimattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> état de début; se dit not. de la voyelle ‘<i>a</i>’ dans le <a href="#entry-pra_nava" class="skt-link">praṇava</a> <a href="#entry-o_m" class="skt-link">oṃ</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aadimat" class="skt-link">ādimat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदिन्</span> <i>ādin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ad_1" class="skt-link">ad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādinī</i> qui mange, qui dévore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadinaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādināga</i> [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] <a href="#entry-aadinaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Ādināga, Serpent Primordial, ép. de <a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a>, de son incarnation <a href="#entry-Ucaraka" class="skt-link">Caraka</a>, et de <a href="#entry-Upata_njali" class="skt-link">Patañjali</a>; on le représente avec un buste anthropomorphe et un corps de serpent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadinaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādinātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-aadinaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. d'Ādinātha «Maître primordial», 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est jaune, le taureau est son symbole; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-U_r_sabhanaatha" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabhanātha</a>, il aurait créé la religion Jaïne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. d'Ādinātha_1, premier maître de l'ordre des <a href="#entry-naathayogin" class="skt-link">nāthayogin</a>; on lui attribue la <a href="#entry-Ukhecariividyaa" class="skt-link">Khecarīvidyā</a>, ouvrage de <a href="#entry-ha_thayoga" class="skt-link">haṭhayoga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadiparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādiparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-aadiparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «du commencement».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadipuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādipurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aadipuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Ādipurāṇa_1 «Primordial récit d'antan», épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Brahmapurāṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de l'Ādipurāṇa_2, contant les vies des 23 saints jaïnes, commencé par Jinasena, terminé par son disciple <a href="#entry-Ugu_nabhadra" class="skt-link">Guṇabhadra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadiraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādirāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-aadiraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. roi mythique, roi d'autrefois; not. <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>, <a href="#entry-Up_rthu" class="skt-link">Pṛthu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadis_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādisṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-s_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">sṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-aadis_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. réalité surnaturelle du mythe, opérant à un autre niveau que la réalité naturelle [<a href="#entry-s_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">sṛṣṭi</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadisiddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādisiddha</i> [<a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadisiddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadisiddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadisiddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādisiddhā</i> phil. parfait de toute éternité (se dit de la conscience).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadistotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādistotra</i> [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] <a href="#entry-aadistotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. hymne fondamental.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaditya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदित्य</span> <i>āditya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aditi" class="skt-link">aditi</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. radieux, dieu souverain, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; il y a d'abord 6 radieux [<i>ādityās</i>]: <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaryamaa" class="skt-link">Aryamā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhaga" class="skt-link">Bhaga</a>, <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ua_mza" class="skt-link">Aṃśa</a>, auxquels se sont adjoints plus tard <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a>, <a href="#entry-Upuu_saa" class="skt-link">Pūṣā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvivasvaan" class="skt-link">Vivasvān</a>, <a href="#entry-Usavitaa" class="skt-link">Savitā</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzakra" class="skt-link">Śakra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Āditya fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a>, le Soleil personnifié en 12 aspects radieux ou positions célestes; cf. <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uravi" class="skt-link">Ravi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] le Soleil comme régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] du sens de la vue [<a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadityavar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādityavarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadityavar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadityavar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadityavar_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādityavarṇā</i> de la couleur du Soleil.<br> <i>vedāham</i> <i>etaṃ</i> <i>puruṣaṃ</i> <i>mahāntam</i> <i>ādityavarṇaṃ</i> <i>tamasaḥ</i> <i>parastāt</i> véd. Je connais cet Être supérieur, couleur du Soleil, au-delà des ténèbres.<br> <a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadiz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आदिश्</span> <i>ādiś</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ādiśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ādiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ādeśya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>prati</i><i>)</i> montrer, enseigner, indiquer; déclarer, annoncer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prescrire, commander — ca. <i>(ādeśayati)</i> indiquer, montrer, dire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadizakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādiśakti</i> [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] <a href="#entry-aadizakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. d'Ādiśakti, forme primordiale de la Déesse; cf. <a href="#entry-Umafgalaa" class="skt-link">Maṅgalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadize_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādiśeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">śeṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-aadize_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ādiśeṣa «Vestige primordial»; cf. <a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadvaadazam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आद्वादशम्</span> <i>ādvādaśam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-dvaadazan" class="skt-link">dvādaśan</a>] adv. jusqu'à douze.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आद्या</span> <i>ādyā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-aadya_2" class="skt-link">ādya_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aadyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. d'Ādyā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «qui est en tête».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadyaabiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādyābīja</i> [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] <a href="#entry-aadyaabiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] le <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<a href="#entry-krii_m" class="skt-link">krīṃ</a>’.<br> <a href="#entry-aadyaa" class="skt-link">ādyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadyanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādyanta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aadyanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādyantā</i> commençant et finissant par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. finissant par ... etc. — <a href="#entry-aadyanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>ādyantau</i> le début et la fin
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadyantavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādyantavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadyantavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyantavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyantavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādyantavatī</i> [«qui a un début et une fin»] fini — adv. comme si c'était le début et la fin.<br> <a href="#entry-aadyanta" class="skt-link">ādyanta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aadyudaatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ādyudātta</i> [<a href="#entry-udaatta" class="skt-link">udātta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aadyudaatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyudaatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aadyudaattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ādyudāttā</i> gram. qui porte l'accent initial.<br> <a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aafgavidya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आङ्गविद्य</span> <i>āṅgavidya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-afgavidyaa" class="skt-link">aṅgavidyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aafgavidya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgavidya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṅgavidyā</i> soc. qui connaît ou étudie la chiromancie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aafgiirasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आङ्गीरस</span> <i>āṅgīrasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-afgiras" class="skt-link">aṅgiras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aafgiirasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgiirasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgiirasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṅgīrasī</i> myth. descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-afgirasas" class="skt-link">aṅgirasas</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Āṅgīrasa, personnifiant l'an 6 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aafgika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आङ्गिक</span> <i>āṅgika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aafgika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṅgikā</i> (sentiment) exprimé par le mouvement du corps ou la gestuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aafgirasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आङ्गिरस</span> <i>āṅgirasa</i> var. <i>āṅgīrasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-afgiras" class="skt-link">aṅgiras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aafgirasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgirasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aafgirasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āṅgirasī</i> myth. descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-afgirasas" class="skt-link">aṅgirasas</a> — <a href="#entry-aafgirasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. not. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. la planère Jupiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagaamika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgāmika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagaamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagaamika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagaamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgāmikā</i> concernant le futur.<br> <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगामिन्</span> <i>āgāmin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagaamin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagaaminii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgāminī</i> qui vient, qui arrive; à venir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. part du <a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a> à venir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगार</span> <i>āgāra</i> var. <i>agāra</i> <a href="#entry-aagaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> logement, habitation, maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagaaradaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgāradāha</i> [<a href="#entry-daaha" class="skt-link">dāha</a>] <a href="#entry-aagaaradaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> incendie d'une habitation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagaaradaahin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgāradāhin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-aagaaradaahin" class="skt-link">m.</a> incendiaire.<br> <a href="#entry-aagaaradaaha" class="skt-link">āgāradāha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aagaara" class="skt-link">āgāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagacchat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगच्छत्</span> <i>āgacchat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagacchat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagacchat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagacchantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgacchantī</i> venant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगम्</span> <i>āgam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āgacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āgata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> arriver à ou dans; venir de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aller vers; survenir, se produire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avoir recours à; tomber dans, encourir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगम</span> <i>āgama</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgamā</i> qui arrive, qui se rapproche; qui aborde — <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrivée, approche; acquisition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moyen, accès; origine, tradition, savoir; autorité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. texte religieux; écriture sacrée, not. <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> d'Inde du Sud ou <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. juxtaposition, affixation; opp. <a href="#entry-aadeza" class="skt-link">ādeśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. augment, accrément (par préfixe de la particule privative ‘<a href="#entry-a" class="skt-link">a</a>/<a href="#entry-an_1" class="skt-link">an_1</a>’); modification d'un thème en gén. — <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>śaivāgama</i>] lit. [«ce qui provient de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>»] <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a> shivaïte sous la forme d'un dialogue où <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> répond à des questions de <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>; on en dénombre traditionnellement 28; opp. <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>pañcarātrāgama</i>] lit. <a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a> vishnouite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. recueil de la Loi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. recueil canonique <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>.<br> <i>varṣāgame</i> adv. à l'approche de la mousson; la mousson étant venue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagama_dambara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgamaḍambara</i> [<a href="#entry-_dambara" class="skt-link">ḍambara</a>] <a href="#entry-aagama_dambara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Āgamaḍambara «Beaucoup de bruit à propos de la religion», pièce satirique de <a href="#entry-Ujayanta_1" class="skt-link">Jayanta_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, montrant l'hypocrisie des sectes religieuses et leur influence politique sous le roi <a href="#entry-Uzafkaravarmaa" class="skt-link">Śaṅkaravarmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगमन</span> <i>āgamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] <a href="#entry-aagamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> approche, arrivée; retour.<br> <i>punar</i> <i>āgamana</i> retour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagamapraamaa_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgamaprāmāṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-praamaa_nya" class="skt-link">prāmāṇya</a>] <a href="#entry-aagamapraamaa_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āgamaprāmāṇya, œuvre phil. due à <a href="#entry-Uyamunaacaarya" class="skt-link">Yamunācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagantu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगन्तु</span> <i>āgantu</i> [<a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] <a href="#entry-aagantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrivée; nouvel arrivant, hôte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगस्</span> <i>āgas</i> <a href="#entry-aagas" class="skt-link">n.</a> péché, faute, offense, mal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagask_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgaskṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagask_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagask_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagask_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgaskṛtā</i> malfaisant.<br> <a href="#entry-aagas" class="skt-link">āgas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगत</span> <i>āgata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgatā</i> survenu, arrivé — <a href="#entry-aagata" class="skt-link">n.</a> action d'arriver, de se produire; avènement, production <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> événement, chose arrivée ou qui s'est produite; accident.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आगति</span> <i>āgati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aagam" class="skt-link">āgam</a>] <a href="#entry-aagati" class="skt-link">f.</a> arrivée; avènement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> origine, création.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagh_r_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आघृणि</span> <i>āghṛṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-gh_r" class="skt-link">ghṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagh_r_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagh_r_ni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagh_r_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui luit, qui rayonne; brillant, radieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आघात</span> <i>āghāta</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aahan" class="skt-link">āhan</a>] <a href="#entry-aaghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> coup, meurtre, destruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> douleur, meurtissure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>āghātasthāna</i>] endroit d'exécution, abattoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaghaatasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āghātasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-aaghaatasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> endroit d'exécution, abattoir.<br> <a href="#entry-aaghaata" class="skt-link">āghāta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaghra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आघ्र</span> <i>āghra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aaghraa" class="skt-link">āghrā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaghra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaghra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaghraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āghrā</i> qui flaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaghraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आघ्रा</span> <i>āghrā</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-ghraa" class="skt-link">ghrā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(ājighrati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āghrāta</i><i>)</i> sentir; flairer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaghraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आघ्राण</span> <i>āghrāṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aaghraa" class="skt-link">āghrā</a>] <a href="#entry-aaghraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de sentir (une odeur, un parfum).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagneya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्नेय</span> <i>āgneya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a><a href="#entry--iya" class="skt-link">-iya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagneya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagneya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagneyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgneyī</i> inflammable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> igné; chaud et sec; de caractère ardent (opp. <a href="#entry-saumya" class="skt-link">saumya</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> combustible, inflammable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. qualifie la moitié de l'année commençant par le mois <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> ou au feu sacré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> au Sud-Est <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. cf. <a href="#entry-aagneyaastra" class="skt-link">āgneyāstra</a> — <a href="#entry-aagneya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> — <a href="#entry-aagneya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āgneya <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uagnipuraa_na" class="skt-link">Agnipurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagneyaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āgneyāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-astra" class="skt-link">astra</a>] <a href="#entry-aagneyaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. de l'arme magique Āgneya «missile d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>», arme magique; <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> la reçut d'<a href="#entry-Uaurva" class="skt-link">Aurva</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> la reçut de <a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a>, et s'en servit pour se défendre d'<a href="#entry-Uafgaarapar_na" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraparṇa</a> avant de lui en enseigner l'usage.<br> <a href="#entry-aagneya" class="skt-link">āgneya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagni_s_tomika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्निष्टोमिक</span> <i>āgniṣṭomika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni_s_toma" class="skt-link">agniṣṭoma</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagni_s_tomika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagni_s_tomika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagni_s_tomikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgniṣṭomikī</i> soc. qui étudie l'<a href="#entry-Uagni_s_toma" class="skt-link">Agniṣṭoma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagniidhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्नीध्र</span> <i>āgnīdhra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>-<a href="#entry-dh_r" class="skt-link">dhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aagniidhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. prêtre entretenant le feu sacré; c'est l'un des 3 prêtres qui assistent le prêtre en chef [<a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>] dans le sacrifice védique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'Āgnīdhra «qui entretient l'Autel», fils de <a href="#entry-Upriyavrata" class="skt-link">Priyavrata</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagnika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्निक</span> <i>āgnika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagnika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgnikā</i> véd. concernant la préparation du feu sacrificiel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagnimaaruta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्निमारुत</span> <i>āgnimāruta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>-<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagnimaaruta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnimaaruta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnimaarutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgnimārutā</i> myth. concernant <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> et les <a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a> — <a href="#entry-aagnimaaruta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. patr. d'<a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a> — <a href="#entry-aagnimaaruta" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. litanie adressée au Feu et aux Vents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagnivaaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्निवारुण</span> <i>āgnivāruṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>-<a href="#entry-varu_na" class="skt-link">varuṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aagnivaaru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnivaaru_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aagnivaaru_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āgnivāruṇā</i> myth. concernant <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagrahaaya_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्रहायणी</span> <i>āgrahāyaṇī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agrahaaya_na" class="skt-link">agrahāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aagrahaaya_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. np. d'Āgrahāyaṇī «Meilleure de l'année», épith. de la pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">mārgaśīrṣa</a>; soc. on y pratique un <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a> et on y célèbre l'anniversaire [<a href="#entry-jayantii_2" class="skt-link">jayantī_2</a>] de <a href="#entry-Udattaatreya" class="skt-link">Dattātreya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. épith. de la constellation lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aagraya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आग्रयण</span> <i>āgrayaṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-agra" class="skt-link">agra</a><a href="#entry--ana" class="skt-link">-ana</a>] <a href="#entry-aagraya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. première libation de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> dans le rite de l'<a href="#entry-agni_s_toma" class="skt-link">agniṣṭoma</a> — <a href="#entry-aagraya_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. sacrifice des prémices, à la fin de la saison des pluies.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aah_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहृ</span> <i>āhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-h_r_1" class="skt-link">hṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āharati)</i> pft. <i>(ājahāra)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āhṛta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> amener, apporter; offrir, donner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aller chercher, prendre, saisir; recevoir; employer — ca. <i>(āhārayati)</i> manger.<br> <i>kratum</i> <i>āhṛ</i> offrir un sacrifice.<br> <i>punar</i> <i>āhṛ</i> reprendre.<br> <i>prativākyam</i> <i>āhṛ</i> recevoir une réponse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह</span> <i>āha</i> interj. de reproche, de commande, de renvoi; hé bien, ouste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहार्</span> <i>āhār</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(āhārayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āhārya</i><i>)</i> prendre son repas, manger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहार</span> <i>āhāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhārā</i> ifc. qui procure, qui amène; qui va amener <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui utilise; qui mange — <a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> prise; emploi; prise de nourriture, repas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«ce qu'on prend»] nourriture, aliment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaaraadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhārādi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-aahaaraadi" class="skt-link">n.</a> la nourriture et le reste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaaraarthin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhārārthin</i> [<a href="#entry-arthin" class="skt-link">arthin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahaaraarthin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaaraarthin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaaraarthinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhārārthinī</i> qui quémande sa nourriture.<br> <a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">āhāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaaradaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhāradāna</i> [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>] <a href="#entry-aahaaradaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> don de nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaaraparityaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhāraparityāga</i> [<a href="#entry-parityaaga" class="skt-link">parityāga</a>] <a href="#entry-aahaaraparityaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> renonciation à la nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहारिन्</span> <i>āhārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhāriṇī</i> qui collecte, qui ramasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहार्य</span> <i>āhārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhāryā</i> qu'on doit amener; à prendre — <a href="#entry-aahaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहन्</span> <i>āhan</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-han_1" class="skt-link">han_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(āhanti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āhata</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>āhatya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> frapper; attaquer; tuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> frapper (un tambour, une corde d'instrument musical).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहनस्</span> <i>āhanas</i> a. <a href="#entry-aahanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui gonfle, qui fermente, qui mousse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> écumé; pressé, battu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indécent, obscène.<br> <i>asabhyabhāṣaṇād</i> <i>āhanā</i> <i>iva</i> <i>bhavati</i> En employant des mots vulgaires on devient obscène.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहर</span> <i>āhara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āharā</i> ifc. qui apporte; qui prend ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui s'adonne à ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dont la nature est de donner ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-aahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> offrande.<br> <i>puṣpāhara</i> a. m. n. dont la nature est d'apporter des fleurs.<br> <i>phalāhara</i> a. m. n. dont la nature est de donner des fruits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āharaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aah_r" class="skt-link">āhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aahara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> transport; action d'amener, d'apporter, de prendre, de se charger.<br> <a href="#entry-aahara" class="skt-link">āhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहत</span> <i>āhata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aahan" class="skt-link">āhan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhatā</i> frappé; atteint; émis (son).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहति</span> <i>āhati</i> [<a href="#entry-aahan" class="skt-link">āhan</a>] <a href="#entry-aahati" class="skt-link">f.</a> coup.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहत्य</span> <i>āhatya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-aahan" class="skt-link">āhan</a>] ind. ayant frappé — adv. en tout, au total.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahavabhuumi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhavabhūmi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] <a href="#entry-aahavabhuumi" class="skt-link">f.</a> champ de bataille.<br> <a href="#entry-aahava_1" class="skt-link">āhava_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhavana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aahavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. oblation.<br> <a href="#entry-aahava_2" class="skt-link">āhava_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahavaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहवनीय</span> <i>āhavanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-aahu" class="skt-link">āhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahavaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahavaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahavaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhavanīyā</i> propre à l'oblation — <a href="#entry-aahavaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. foyer propre aux offrandes du sacrifice védique, à l'est de l'aire sacrée [<a href="#entry-vedi" class="skt-link">vedi</a>]; on le ranime deux fois par jour, à partir du foyer domestique [<a href="#entry-gaarhapatya" class="skt-link">gārhapatya</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvaizvaanara" class="skt-link">Vaiśvānara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahavaniiyaagni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhavanīyāgni</i> [<a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>] <a href="#entry-aahavaniiyaagni" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. feu du sacrifice.<br> <a href="#entry-aahavaniiya" class="skt-link">āhavanīya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहित</span> <i>āhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aadhaa" class="skt-link">ādhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhitā</i> placé sur, déposé; effectué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahitaagni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhitāgni</i> [<a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahitaagni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahitaagni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahitaagni" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a établi le feu (du sacrifice) — <a href="#entry-aahitaagni" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«celui qui a mis ses feux en place»] véd. maître de maison [<a href="#entry-g_rhastha" class="skt-link">gṛhastha</a>] ayant offert des sacrifices solennels requérant les trois feux; il doit avoir un fils, mais être encore jeune.<br> <a href="#entry-aahita" class="skt-link">āhita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahitalak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhitalakṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahitalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahitalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahitalak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhitalakṣaṇā</i> renommé — ifc. connu comme; appelé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahitu_n_dika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहितुण्डिक</span> <i>āhituṇḍika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ahitu_n_da" class="skt-link">ahituṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-aahitu_n_dika" class="skt-link">m.</a> montreur de serpent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahlaad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्लाद्</span> <i>āhlād</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-hlaad" class="skt-link">hlād</a>] ca. <i>(hlādayati)</i> rafraîchir, raviver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahlaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्लाद</span> <i>āhlāda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aahlaad" class="skt-link">āhlād</a>] <a href="#entry-aahlaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> bien-être; fraîcheur, bonheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahlaadaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्लादक</span> <i>āhlādaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aahlaad" class="skt-link">āhlād</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahlaadaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahlaadaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahlaadikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhlādikā</i> rafraîchissant, vivifiant; plaisant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्न</span> <i>āhna</i> [<a href="#entry-ahan" class="skt-link">ahan</a>] <a href="#entry-aahna" class="skt-link">n.</a> période de plusieurs jours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahnika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhnika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahnika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahnika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahnikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhnikā</i> diurne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> quotidien — <a href="#entry-aahnika" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. rite quotidien à heure fixe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> journée de travail; ration quotidienne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce qu'on peut lire en un jour; chapitre d'un livre; section d'un chapitre [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>]; not. l'une des 85 sections du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aahna" class="skt-link">āhna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaho">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहो</span> <i>āho</i> interj. de doute — conj. ou (dans phrase interr.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahosvid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āhosvid</i> [<a href="#entry-svid_1" class="skt-link">svid_1</a>] conj. ou bien?<br> <i>kim</i> <i>īśvarānapekṣitam</i> <i>āhosvid</i> <i>īśvarāpekṣitam</i> Est-ce indépendant de Dieu ou bien dépendant de Dieu?<br> <a href="#entry-aaho" class="skt-link">āho</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहु</span> <i>āhu</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-hu" class="skt-link">hu</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(ājuhoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āhuta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āhavanīya</i><i>)</i> offrir dans ⟨loc.⟩ (not. le feu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहुक</span> <i>āhuka</i> [<a href="#entry-aahu" class="skt-link">āhu</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-aahuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi <a href="#entry-v_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">vṛṣṇi</a> Āhuka «Maître des offrandes»; il est père de <a href="#entry-Udevaka" class="skt-link">Devaka</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uugrasena" class="skt-link">Ugrasena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहुत</span> <i>āhuta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aahu" class="skt-link">āhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhutā</i> offert dans le feu; déposé sur le bûcher (cadavre) — <a href="#entry-aahuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> oblation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहुति</span> <i>āhuti</i> [<a href="#entry-aahu" class="skt-link">āhu</a>] <a href="#entry-aahuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> oblation, offrande; fait d'offrir l'oblation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहू</span> <i>āhū</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-huu" class="skt-link">hū</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āhvayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āhūta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>āhūya</i><i>)</i> appeler, convoquer; mander, inviter — pr. md. <i>(āhvayate)</i> défier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आहूत</span> <i>āhūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aahuu" class="skt-link">āhū</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aahuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aahuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aahuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āhūtā</i> appelé, convoqué; invoqué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahvaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्वान</span> <i>āhvāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aahuu" class="skt-link">āhū</a>] <a href="#entry-aahvaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> appel, invocation; invitation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> appel à comparaître; défi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aahvaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आह्वरक</span> <i>āhvaraka</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-hvaraka" class="skt-link">hvaraka</a>] <a href="#entry-aahvaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. les hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आज</span> <i>āja</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aja_1" class="skt-link">aja_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājā</i> issu de la chèvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājādi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-aajaadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> chèvres, etc..
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaadimaa_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājādimāṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-aajaadimaa_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. nourriture carnée (interdite aux ascètes).<br> <a href="#entry-aajaadi" class="skt-link">ājādi</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aaja" class="skt-link">āja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजान</span> <i>ājāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aajan" class="skt-link">ājan</a>] <a href="#entry-aajaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> naissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaaneya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājāneya</i> [<a href="#entry--iya" class="skt-link">-iya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aajaaneya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaaneya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaaneyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājāneyī</i> de noble naissance — <a href="#entry-aajaaneya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheval de race.<br> <a href="#entry-aajaana" class="skt-link">ājāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaanika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājānika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aajaanika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaanika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaanikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājānikā</i> originel, natif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजानु</span> <i>ājānu</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-jaanu" class="skt-link">jānu</a>] adv. jusqu'aux genoux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaanubaahu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājānubāhu</i> [<a href="#entry-baahu" class="skt-link">bāhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aajaanubaahu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaanubaahu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajaanubaahu" class="skt-link">f.</a> dont les bras descendent jusqu'au genou.<br> <a href="#entry-aajaanu" class="skt-link">ājānu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजाति</span> <i>ājāti</i> [<a href="#entry-aajan" class="skt-link">ājan</a>] <a href="#entry-aajaati" class="skt-link">f.</a> naissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजन्</span> <i>ājan</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-jan" class="skt-link">jan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(ājāyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ājāta</i><i>)</i> naître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajanma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजन्म</span> <i>ājanma</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-janman" class="skt-link">janman</a>] adv. de naissance, depuis la naissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajanmabhaa_sit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājanmabhāṣitṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sit_r" class="skt-link">bhāṣitṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aajanmabhaa_sit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajanmabhaa_sit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajanmabhaa_sitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājanmabhāṣitrī</i> locuteur natif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajanmazuddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājanmaśuddha</i> [<a href="#entry-zuddha" class="skt-link">śuddha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aajanmazuddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajanmazuddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aajanmazuddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājanmaśuddhā</i> pur depuis sa naissance.<br> <a href="#entry-aajanma" class="skt-link">ājanma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajarasam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजरसम्</span> <i>ājarasam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-jaras" class="skt-link">jaras</a>] adv. jusqu'à la vieillesse — dat. <i>ājarasāya</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajibhuumi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājibhūmi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] <a href="#entry-aajibhuumi" class="skt-link">f.</a> champ de bataille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajiiv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजीव्</span> <i>ājīv</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ājīvati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ājīvate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ājīvita</i><i>)</i> vivre de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajiiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजीव</span> <i>ājīva</i> [<a href="#entry-aajiiv" class="skt-link">ājīv</a>] <a href="#entry-aajiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> moyen d'existence; manière de vivre.<br> <i>ājīvam</i> adv. pour la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajiivaantam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजीवान्तम्</span> <i>ājīvāntam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-jiivaanta" class="skt-link">jīvānta</a>] adv. jusqu'à la mort; pour toute la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajiivika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आजीविक</span> <i>ājīvika</i> [<a href="#entry-aajiiv" class="skt-link">ājīv</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-aajiivika" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [«qui suit les règles de vie»] membre d'une secte non orthodoxe de renonçants matérialistes fondée par <a href="#entry-Ugozaala" class="skt-link">Gośāla</a>; leur doctrine fataliste était basée sur le strict déterminisme du destin [<a href="#entry-niyati" class="skt-link">niyati</a>] — <a href="#entry-aajiivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājīvikā</i> subsistance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajiziras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājiśiras</i> [<a href="#entry-ziras" class="skt-link">śiras</a>] <a href="#entry-aajiziras" class="skt-link">n.</a> front (armée).<br> <a href="#entry-aaji_2" class="skt-link">āji_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आज्य</span> <i>ājya</i> [<a href="#entry-aa_nj" class="skt-link">āñj</a>] <a href="#entry-aajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. beurre fondu (pour la libation), chrème; syn. <a href="#entry-gh_rta" class="skt-link">ghṛta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajyaahuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājyāhuti</i> [<a href="#entry-aahuti" class="skt-link">āhuti</a>] <a href="#entry-aajyaahuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. oblation de beurre fondu; syn. <a href="#entry-aajyahoma" class="skt-link">ājyahoma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aajya" class="skt-link">ājya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajyahoma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājyahoma</i> [<a href="#entry-homa" class="skt-link">homa</a>] <a href="#entry-aajyahoma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. oblation de beurre fondu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aajyaze_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājyaśeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">śeṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-aajyaze_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aajyaze_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. reliquat de beurre fondu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaj_naacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ājñācakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-aaj_naacakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> tantr., situé entre les deux sourcils [<a href="#entry-bhruumadhya" class="skt-link">bhrūmadhya</a>]; il est représenté par un lotus blanc de 2 pétales; un triangle blanc y symbolise l'esprit, et porte le <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<a href="#entry-om" class="skt-link">om</a>’; ce <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> contrôle le mental; il est le centre psychique de l'Autorité de la Vérité.<br> <a href="#entry-aaj_naa_2" class="skt-link">ājñā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaj_napta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आज्ञप्त</span> <i>ājñapta</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-aaj_naa_1" class="skt-link">ājñā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaj_napta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaj_napta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaj_naptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ājñaptā</i> commandé, ordonné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaj_napti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आज्ञप्ति</span> <i>ājñapti</i> [<a href="#entry-aaj_napta" class="skt-link">ājñapta</a>] <a href="#entry-aaj_napti" class="skt-link">f.</a> commande, ordre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकृ</span> <i>ākṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(ākaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākṛta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> rapprocher; appeler, invoquer — ps. <i>(ākriyate)</i> prendre forme — ca. <i>(ākārayati)</i> faire venir, créer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकृष्</span> <i>ākṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ākarṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> attirer, tirer vers soi, amener à soi; tirer (not. la flèche sur la corde de l'arc) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> extraire (de ⟨abl.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séduire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकृष्ट</span> <i>ākṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aak_r_s" class="skt-link">ākṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aak_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aak_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aak_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākṛṣṭā</i> tiré (glaive), bandé (arc).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकॄ</span> <i>ākṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rr" class="skt-link">kṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ākirati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākīrṇa</i><i>)</i> répandre sur, verser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकृति</span> <i>ākṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aak_r" class="skt-link">ākṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aak_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> forme, extérieur, aspect; apparence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. forme générique, genre, classe, type; syn. <a href="#entry-jaati" class="skt-link">jāti</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-vyakti" class="skt-link">vyakti</a>.<br> <i>śobhanākṛti</i> joli d'apparence.<br> <i>subhagākṛti</i> heureux en apparence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_rtiga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākṛtigaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aak_rtiga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aak_rtiga_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aak_rtiga_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākṛtigaṇā</i> gram. paradigmatique, générique — <a href="#entry-aak_rtiga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. classe de mots, nommée par un membre caractéristique, comme <a href="#entry-p_r_sodara" class="skt-link">pṛṣodara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. classe de mots ayant une spécificité grammaticale désignée par une particularité formelle; opp. <a href="#entry-paripuur_naga_na" class="skt-link">paripūrṇagaṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aak_rti" class="skt-link">ākṛti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_sepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आक्षेप</span> <i>ākṣepa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aak_sip" class="skt-link">ākṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-aak_sepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> enlèvement, arrachement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offense, blâme; objection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. fait de postuler; ellipse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> geste de la main, utilisé par les récitants véd. pour noter l'accent <a href="#entry-svarita" class="skt-link">svarita</a>.<br> <i>mama</i> <i>tu</i> <i>ākṣepaḥ</i> <i>nāsti</i> je n'y ai pas d'objection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_sepasamaadhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākṣepasamādhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-samaadhaana" class="skt-link">samādhāna</a>] <a href="#entry-aak_sepasamaadhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. rhét. réponse aux objections, technique de débat scholastique.<br> <a href="#entry-aak_sepa" class="skt-link">ākṣepa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aak_sip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आक्षिप्</span> <i>ākṣip</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ākṣipati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākṣipta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> enlever; tirer à soi; tirer de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> railler; provoquer, défier — ca. <i>(ākṣepayati)</i> faire verser; renverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaafk_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाङ्क्ष्</span> <i>ākāṅkṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-kaafk_s" class="skt-link">kāṅkṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ākāṅkṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākāṅkṣita</i><i>)</i> désirer, souhaiter; aspirer à ⟨acc. g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. requérir des actants supplémentaires pour avoir un sens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaafk_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाङ्क्ष</span> <i>ākāṅkṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aakaafk_s" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāṅkṣā</i> gram. qui requiert des actants supplémentaires pour avoir un sens — <a href="#entry-aakaafk_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāṅkṣā</i> désir, souhait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. dépendance sémantique; valence; présupposition; [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] on distingue les dépendances mutuelles [<a href="#entry-utthita" class="skt-link">utthita</a>] et uni-directionnelles [<a href="#entry-utthaapya_1" class="skt-link">utthāpya_1</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaafk_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाङ्क्षिन्</span> <i>ākāṅkṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aakaafk_s" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣ</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāṅkṣiṇī</i> qui désire, qui espère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaafk_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाङ्क्ष्य</span> <i>ākāṅkṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aakaafk_s" class="skt-link">ākāṅkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaafk_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāṅkṣyā</i> désirable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकारण</span> <i>ākāraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aak_r" class="skt-link">ākṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aakaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> rassemblement; appel, invocation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaaraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākārānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaaraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaaraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaaraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākārāntā</i> gram. (mot) terminé par ‘<i>ā</i>’.<br> <a href="#entry-aakaara_2" class="skt-link">ākāra_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaaravat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākāravat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaaravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaaravat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaaravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāravatī</i> bien formé, bien fait, de bon aspect.<br> <a href="#entry-aakaara_1" class="skt-link">ākāra_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाश्</span> <i>ākāś</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-kaaz" class="skt-link">kāś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ākāśate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākāśita</i><i>)</i> regarder, reconnaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आकाश</span> <i>ākāśa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aakaaz" class="skt-link">ākāś</a>] <a href="#entry-aakaaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«visibilité»] espace, air, ciel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'Espace ou Éther, l'une des 9 substances [<a href="#entry-dravya_1" class="skt-link">dravya_1</a>] du <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>, l'un des 5 éléments [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] du <a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>; son <a href="#entry-pa_ncabhuutasthala" class="skt-link">pañcabhūtasthala</a> est <a href="#entry-Ucidambara" class="skt-link">Cidambara</a>-<a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'Impulsion primordiale (proprement l'éveil de la manifestation) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. zéro <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. (communication) sans fil, radiodiffusion; ondes, rayonnement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazacaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākāśacārin</i> [<a href="#entry-caarin" class="skt-link">cārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaazacaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaazacaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaazacaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāśacāriṇī</i> qui se déplace dans les airs — <a href="#entry-aakaazacaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> oiseau; planète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazagarbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākāśagarbha</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>] <a href="#entry-aakaazagarbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. [Vajrayāna] np. de l'accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] Ākāśagarbha «Réceptacle de Vide»; on l'invoque pour améliorer sa mémoire et ses capacités intellectuelles, et pour obtenir l'habileté manuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ākāśaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aakaazaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaazaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aakaazajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ākāśajā</i> issu du ciel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazamudraa">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazavaa_nii">

[«voix des airs»] radio.<br> <i>iyam</i> <i>ākāśavāṇī</i> mod. ici la radio.<br> <a href="#entry-aakaaza" class="skt-link">ākāśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazavacana">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazavat">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakaazayaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

[<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(ākarṇayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākarṇita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ākarṇya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>ākarṇya</i><i>)</i> entendre; écouter, prêter l'oreille; tendre l'oreille.<br> <i>ākarṇam</i> adv. (flèche tirée) depuis l'oreille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aakar_namuulam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  </div>

  </div>

[<a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a>] forme verbale finie; opp. <a href="#entry-dhaatu" class="skt-link">dhātu</a> (racine).

  </div>
</div>
