[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ākrāmati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākrānta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>ākramya</i><i>)</i> s'approcher de, aller vers, rendre visite à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fouler aux pieds; peser sur ⟨acc.⟩; saisir, attaquer — ca. <i>(ākrāmayati)</i> faire entrer.
  </div>

[<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(ākulayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākulita</i><i>)</i> remplir de confusion, troubler.<br> <i>ākulam</i> adv. dans la confusion, avec trouble.

[<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] l'activité [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] de contraction.

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलभ्</span> <i>ālabh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-labh" class="skt-link">labh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ālabhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālabdha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i><i>)</i> prendre en main, tenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tuer; sacrifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obtenir, atteindre; se concilier, gagner qqn.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalabhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलभ्य</span> <i>ālabhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aalabh" class="skt-link">ālabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalabhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalabhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalabhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālabhyā</i> à tenir; à sacrifier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalamb">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलम्ब्</span> <i>ālamb</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lamb" class="skt-link">lamb</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ālambate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālambita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> pendre de, s'appuyer sur ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saisir, tenir, s'accrocher à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलम्ब</span> <i>ālamba</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aalamb" class="skt-link">ālamb</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalamba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalambaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālambā</i> qui pend — <a href="#entry-aalamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> support; appui; réceptacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. perpendiculaire.<br> <i>ālambam</i> adv. en portant; en supportant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalambana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलम्बन</span> <i>ālambana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aalamb" class="skt-link">ālamb</a>] <a href="#entry-aalambana" class="skt-link">n.</a> support, point d'appui; fondation, base <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] cause nécessaire d'un effet, not. d'une sensation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. attribut d'un objet contribuant à sa perception par l'un des sens: forme, son, odeur, goût et texture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलम्भ</span> <i>ālambha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aalabh" class="skt-link">ālabh</a>] <a href="#entry-aalambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de saisir, de toucher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> action de déraciner (plante) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mise à mort; sacrifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalambin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलम्बिन्</span> <i>ālambin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aalamb" class="skt-link">ālamb</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalambin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalambin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalambinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālambinī</i> appuyé sur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलप्</span> <i>ālap</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lap" class="skt-link">lap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ālapati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālapita</i><i>)</i> parler, dire à ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(ālāpayati)</i> engager la conversation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलपित</span> <i>ālapita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aalap" class="skt-link">ālap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālapitā</i> entretenu — <a href="#entry-aalapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> entretien avec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलस्य</span> <i>ālasya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-alasa" class="skt-link">alasa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aalasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> paresse, molesse, manque d'énergie.<br> <i>ālasyaṃ</i> <i>hi</i> <i>manuṣyāṇāṃ</i> <i>śarīrastho</i> <i>mahān</i> <i>ripuḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>] La paresse est le pire ennemi corporel de l'homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalasyavacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ālasyavacana</i> [<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] <a href="#entry-aalasyavacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«parole de paresseux»] argument fataliste.<br> <a href="#entry-aalasya" class="skt-link">ālasya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलय</span> <i>ālaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aalii" class="skt-link">ālī</a>] <a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> maison, demeure; asile, temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalayavij_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ālayavijñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-vij_naana" class="skt-link">vijñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-aalayavij_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. [<a href="#entry-vij_naanavaada" class="skt-link">vijñānavāda</a>] connaissance du soi.<br> <a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">ālaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalekhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलेख्य</span> <i>ālekhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aalikh" class="skt-link">ālikh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalekhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalekhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalekhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālekhyā</i> à écrire; à peindre — <a href="#entry-aalekhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> écriture, peinture; portrait; le dessin, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalih">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलिह्</span> <i>ālih</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lih_1" class="skt-link">lih_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(āleḍhi)</i> pr. md. <i>(ālīḍhe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālīḍha</i><i>)</i> lécher, laper.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आली</span> <i>ālī</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lii" class="skt-link">lī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(ālīyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālīna</i><i>)</i> demeurer, se cacher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalii_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलीढ</span> <i>ālīḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aalih" class="skt-link">ālih</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalii_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalii_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalii_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālīḍhā</i> léché; lapé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalikh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलिख्</span> <i>ālikh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-likh" class="skt-link">likh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ālikhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālikhita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ālekhya</i><i>)</i> faire un croquis; dessiner, peindre — ps. <i>(ālikhyate)</i> être décrit, être dessiné.<br> <i>nāmūlāllikhyate</i> <i>kiñcit</i> [<a href="#entry-Umallinaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mallinātha_1</a>] On ne doit rien décrire sans fondement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaloc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोच्</span> <i>āloc</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-loc" class="skt-link">loc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ālocate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālocita</i><i>)</i> considérer, réfléchir sur — ca. <i>(ālocayati)</i> montrer; percevoir, regarder; considérer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalocita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोचित</span> <i>ālocita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aaloc" class="skt-link">āloc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalocita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalocita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalocitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālocitā</i> considéré, examiné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalohita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोहित</span> <i>ālohita</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lohita" class="skt-link">lohita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalohita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalohita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalohitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālohitā</i> rougeâtre, tirant vers le rouge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalok">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोक्</span> <i>ālok</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-lok" class="skt-link">lok</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ālokati)</i> ca. <i>(ālokayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ālokita</i><i>)</i> regarder, contempler; constater.<br> <i>anyaccharaṇaṃ</i> <i>nālokyate</i> il n'y a pas d'autre alternative.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोक</span> <i>āloka</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aalok" class="skt-link">ālok</a>] <a href="#entry-aaloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> vue; lumière, éclairage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. désigne un commentaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalokana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोकन</span> <i>ālokana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aalok" class="skt-link">ālok</a>] <a href="#entry-aalokana" class="skt-link">n.</a> contemplation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalokin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलोकिन्</span> <i>ālokin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aalok" class="skt-link">ālok</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aalokin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aalokin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aalokinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ālokinī</i> qui regarde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aalu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आलु</span> <i>ālu</i> <a href="#entry-aalu" class="skt-link">m.</a> ébène — <a href="#entry-aalu" class="skt-link">n.</a> racine comestible; patate douce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. pomme de terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaluka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āluka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-aaluka" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Dioscorea alata</i>, patate douce.<br> <a href="#entry-aalu" class="skt-link">ālu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aam_rd">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमृद्</span> <i>āmṛd</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(āmṛdnāti)</i> broyer, écraser; détruire en frottant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aam_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमृश्</span> <i>āmṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-m_rz" class="skt-link">mṛś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(āmṛśati)</i> toucher, tâter; goûter; jouir (not. d'une femme) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> considérer, réfléchir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम</span> <i>āma</i> [<a href="#entry-aam_2" class="skt-link">ām_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmā</i> cru [«pas cuit»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vert [«pas mûr»] (opp. <a href="#entry-pakva" class="skt-link">pakva</a>) — <a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">m.</a> maladie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ωμος; lat. <i>amārus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamaavaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमावास्य</span> <i>āmāvāsya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-amaavaasyaa" class="skt-link">amāvāsyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamaavaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamaavaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamaavaasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmāvāsyā</i> relatif à la nouvelle lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> né sous la nouvelle lune — <a href="#entry-aamaavaasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. oblation de nouvelle lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamaazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmāśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">āśaya</a>] <a href="#entry-aamaazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. estomac.<br> <a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">āma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamalaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमलक</span> <i>āmalaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-amla" class="skt-link">amla</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-aamalaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamalakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmalakī</i> natu. bot. <i>Emblica officinalis</i>, arbre myrobolan; syn. <a href="#entry-dhaatrii" class="skt-link">dhātrī</a> — <a href="#entry-aamalaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>āmalakaphala</i>] fruit du myrobolan, groseille à maquerau indienne; sa transparence est utilisée en métaphore de vision extralucide not. du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; méd. on l'utilise not. pour traiter la bile [<a href="#entry-pitta" class="skt-link">pitta</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-triphalaa" class="skt-link">triphalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. motif couronnant le <a href="#entry-zikhara" class="skt-link">śikhara</a> de certains temples de l'Inde du Nord (en forme du fruit d'<a href="#entry-aamalaka" class="skt-link">āmalaka</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमन्त्र</span> <i>āmantra</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(āmantrayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āmantrita</i><i>)</i> adresser la parole à, saluer, parler à; sommer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dire au revoir, prendre congé.<br> <i>āmantrya</i> ind. ayant payé ses respects à, ayant salué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ayant pris congé de.<br> <i>āmantrayasva</i> <i>sahacaram</i> dis au-revoir à ton ami.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamantra_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमन्त्रण</span> <i>āmantraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aamantra" class="skt-link">āmantra</a>] <a href="#entry-aamantra_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> adresse, invitation; accueil, bienvenue; (formule de) salut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamantrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमन्त्रित</span> <i>āmantrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aamantra" class="skt-link">āmantra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamantrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamantrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamantritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmantritā</i> adressé, à qui l'on parle; de qui l'on prend congé — <a href="#entry-aamantrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> adresse; entretien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. vocatif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamarakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmarakta</i> [<a href="#entry-rakta" class="skt-link">rakta</a>] <a href="#entry-aamarakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. dysenterie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamarda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमर्द</span> <i>āmarda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aam_rd" class="skt-link">āmṛd</a>] <a href="#entry-aamarda" class="skt-link">m.</a> écrasement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamardaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमर्दक</span> <i>āmardaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aam_rd" class="skt-link">āmṛd</a>] <a href="#entry-aamardaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> «qui détruit» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. d'Āmardaka, fondateur d'une secte <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> dualiste (8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il fonda un monastère [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] éponyme à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamarza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमर्श</span> <i>āmarśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aam_rz" class="skt-link">āmṛś</a>] <a href="#entry-aamarza" class="skt-link">m.</a> contact <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proximité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamavaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmavāta</i> [<a href="#entry-vaata" class="skt-link">vāta</a>] <a href="#entry-aamavaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. douleur intestinale, flatulence; constipation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमय</span> <i>āmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">āma</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> maladie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamayitnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमयित्नु</span> <i>āmayitnu</i> [<a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">āma</a><a href="#entry--yitnu" class="skt-link">-yitnu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamayitnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamayitnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamayitnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui rend malade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamazuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmaśūla</i> [<a href="#entry-zuula" class="skt-link">śūla</a>] <a href="#entry-aamazuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. colique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aami_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमिष</span> <i>āmiṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">āma</a>-<a href="#entry-i_saa" class="skt-link">iṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-aami_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> viande, chair; nourriture, proie; butin désiré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> objet de désir; objet plaisant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désir ardent, passion.<br> <i>siṃhavyāghrāmiṣīkṛ</i> être une proie pour les lions et les tigres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aami_saazin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmiṣāśin</i> [<a href="#entry-aazin" class="skt-link">āśin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aami_saazin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aami_saazin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aami_saazinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmiṣāśinī</i> carnivore.<br> <a href="#entry-aami_sa" class="skt-link">āmiṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamiik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमीक्षा</span> <i>āmīkṣā</i> <a href="#entry-aamiik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mélange de lait chaud et de caillé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमिस्</span> <i>āmis</i> [<a href="#entry-aama" class="skt-link">āma</a>] <a href="#entry-aamis" class="skt-link">n.</a> chair crue; chair.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमित्र</span> <i>āmitra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-amitra" class="skt-link">amitra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-amitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>amitrī</i> inamical; odieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्ल</span> <i>āmla</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-amla" class="skt-link">amla</a>] <a href="#entry-aamla" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamlaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmlā</i> natu. bot. <i>Tamarindus indica</i>, arbre tamarin — <a href="#entry-aamla" class="skt-link">n.</a> acidité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamnaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्ना</span> <i>āmnā</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-mnaa" class="skt-link">mnā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āmanati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āmnāta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> mentionner, citer; apprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamnaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्नात</span> <i>āmnāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aamnaa" class="skt-link">āmnā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamnaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamnaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamnaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmnātā</i> transmis, enseigné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamnaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्नाय</span> <i>āmnāya</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-aamnaa" class="skt-link">āmnā</a>] <a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«ce qui doit être appris»] doctrine sacrée; <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tradition; enseignement traditionnel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamnaayapii_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmnāyapīṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-pii_tha" class="skt-link">pīṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-aamnaayapii_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> centre d'enseignement religieux; les 4 principaux sont <a href="#entry-puurvaamnaaya" class="skt-link">pūrvāmnāya</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>, <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamnaaya" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāmnāya</a> à <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgeri" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgeri</a>, <a href="#entry-pazcimaamnaaya" class="skt-link">paścimāmnāya</a> à <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>, et <a href="#entry-uttaraamnaaya" class="skt-link">uttarāmnāya</a> à <a href="#entry-Ubadariinaatha" class="skt-link">Badarīnātha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">āmnāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamoda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमोद</span> <i>āmoda</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-moda" class="skt-link">moda</a>] <a href="#entry-aamoda" class="skt-link">m.</a> joie; parfum.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्र</span> <i>āmra</i> <a href="#entry-aamra" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Mangifera indica</i>, manguier — <a href="#entry-aamra" class="skt-link">n.</a> mangue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamrava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āmravaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-aamrava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> bois de manguiers.<br> <a href="#entry-aamra" class="skt-link">āmra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamre_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्रेड्</span> <i>āmreḍ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-mre_d" class="skt-link">mreḍ</a>] ca. <i>(āmreḍayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āmreḍita</i><i>)</i> répéter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamre_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्रेड</span> <i>āmreḍa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aamre_d" class="skt-link">āmreḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-aamre_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> répétition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamre_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आम्रेडित</span> <i>āmreḍita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-aamre_d" class="skt-link">āmreḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamre_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamre_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamre_ditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmreḍitā</i> répété — <a href="#entry-aamre_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. reduplication; mot composé répétitif (par ex. <i>divedive</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमुक्त</span> <i>āmukta</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aamukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aamukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aamuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āmuktā</i> mis (vêtement); habillé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enlevé; déshabillé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lâché; lancé, envoyé — <a href="#entry-aamukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> cuirasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aamuulam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आमूलम्</span> <i>āmūlam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] adv. depuis l'origine; à cœur; à fond, radicalement [«jusqu'à la racine»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aan_r_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनृण्य</span> <i>ānṛṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-an_r_na" class="skt-link">anṛṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aan_r_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de dette; paiement d'une dette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acquittement d'une dette ou d'un devoir sacré; accomplissement d'une promesse due <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assouvissement d'un désir «à cœur joie».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aan_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनृत्</span> <i>ānṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-n_rt" class="skt-link">nṛt</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(ānṛtyati)</i> danser vers; sauter vers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aan_rza_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनृशंस</span> <i>ānṛśaṃsa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-an_rza_msa" class="skt-link">anṛśaṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> bonté, gentillesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aan_rza_msya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānṛśaṃsya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānṛśaṃsyā</i> gentil, doux, bienveillant — <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msya" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur, bonté, gentillesse, compassion.<br> <i>sarveṣām</i> <i>ahiṃsā</i> <i>satyaṃ</i> <i>śaucam</i> <i>anasūyā</i> <i>ānṛśaṃsyaṃ</i> <i>kṣamā</i> <i>ca</i> [KA.] Envers tous la bienveillance, la sincérité, la pureté, l'absence de convoitise, la compassion, et l'indulgence.<br> <a href="#entry-aan_rza_msa" class="skt-link">ānṛśaṃsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन</span> <i>āna</i> [<a href="#entry-an_2" class="skt-link">an_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānā</i> exhalé, soufflé — <a href="#entry-aana" class="skt-link">m.</a> vent, souffle, respiration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> not. exhalation par le nez <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'un des 5 souffles vitaux [<a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-apaana" class="skt-link">apāna</a>, <a href="#entry-udaana" class="skt-link">udāna</a>, <a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-vyaana" class="skt-link">vyāna</a>, <a href="#entry-samaana_1" class="skt-link">samāna_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aana_duha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनडुह</span> <i>ānaḍuha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ana_dvah" class="skt-link">anaḍvah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aana_duha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aana_duha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aana_duhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānaḍuhā</i> taurin; qui concerne le taureau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनक</span> <i>ānaka</i> <a href="#entry-aanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> gros tambour militaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nuage d'orage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanakadundubhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānakadundubhi</i> [<a href="#entry-dundubhi" class="skt-link">dundubhi</a>] <a href="#entry-aanakadundubhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ānakadundubhi «Tambour céleste», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a>, dont la naissance fut saluée par un roulement de tambour divin.<br> <a href="#entry-aanaka" class="skt-link">ānaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनम्</span> <i>ānam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ānamati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ānata</i><i>)</i> se courber, s'incliner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saluer, honorer, rendre hommage à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनन</span> <i>ānana</i> [<a href="#entry-aana" class="skt-link">āna</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aanana" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«ce par quoi l'on respire»] bouche; visage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनन्द्</span> <i>ānand</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ānandati)</i> se réjouir; être heureux — ca. <i>(ānandayati)</i> réjouir, combler de joie — ca. md. <i>(ānandayate)</i> s'amuser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनन्द</span> <i>ānanda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aanand" class="skt-link">ānand</a>] <a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> joie, félicité; plaisir; béatitude, joie extatique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] Joie de l'union Divine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. np. d'Ānanda «Joyeux», disciple du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. d'Ānanda_1 «Béat», personnifiant l'an 48 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>saccidānanda</i>] phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] la Joie dans l'unité <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>-<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandaanugata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandānugata</i> [<a href="#entry-anugata" class="skt-link">anugata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanandaanugata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandaanugata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandaanugataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānandānugatā</i> phil. en état de grâce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandabhaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. d'Ānandabhaṭṭa (16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvallaalacarita" class="skt-link">Vallālacarita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe de l'<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> Ānandabodha (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ ou 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); son <i>magnum opus</i> est le <a href="#entry-Unyaayamakaranda" class="skt-link">Nyāyamakaranda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandacinmaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandacinmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-cinmaya" class="skt-link">cinmaya</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandacinmaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. joie spirituelle pure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Ānandagiri, philosophe du 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uzafkaravijaya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkaravijaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandalaharii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandalaharī</i> [<a href="#entry-laharii" class="skt-link">laharī</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandalaharii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Ānandalaharī «Flot de béatitude», texte de louange [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] dû à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>, en mètre <a href="#entry-zikhari_nii" class="skt-link">śikhariṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanandamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānandamayī</i> fait de félicité; spirituel, extatique — <a href="#entry-aanandamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānandamayī</i> mod. np. de la mystique bengalie Mā Ānandamayī, née Nirmalā Sundarī Devī (1896–1982).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandamayakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandamayakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandamayakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] l'enveloppe spirituelle ou extatique, formant le corps causal ou originel [<a href="#entry-kaara_nazariira" class="skt-link">kāraṇaśarīra</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-aanandamaya" class="skt-link">ānandamaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandataa_n_dava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandatāṇḍava</i> [<a href="#entry-taa_n_dava" class="skt-link">tāṇḍava</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandataa_n_dava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandataa_n_dava" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-nadaanta" class="skt-link">nadānta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandatiirtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandatīrtha</i> [<a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandatiirtha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Ānandatīrtha, épith. du maître <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a> «qui fait accéder à la félicité».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandavana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-aanandavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. d'Ānandavana, grande étable [<a href="#entry-gozaalaa" class="skt-link">gośālā</a>] située à Pathameḍā (mod. district de Barmer, Rajasthan), aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Ugodhaama" class="skt-link">Godhāma</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatiirtha" class="skt-link">mahātīrtha</a>; selon la tradition, elle fut établie par <a href="#entry-Uaniruddha" class="skt-link">Aniruddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandavardhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandavardhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vardhana" class="skt-link">vardhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanandavardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandavardhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandavardhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānandavardhanā</i> qui augmente la joie — <a href="#entry-aanandavardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Ānandavardhana, poète de la cour du roi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> <a href="#entry-Uavantivarmaa" class="skt-link">Avantivarmā</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il composa le traité d'esthétique <a href="#entry-Udhvanyaaloka" class="skt-link">Dhvanyāloka</a>, qui établit que le sens suggéré [<a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>] est l'essence de la poésie, et il est ainsi appelé <a href="#entry-Udhvanikaara" class="skt-link">Dhvanikāra</a> «Inventeur du sens suggéré»; on lui doit aussi le <a href="#entry-Usah_rdayaaloka" class="skt-link">Sahṛdayāloka</a> qui théorise l'émotion esthétique, et le recueil de poèmes dévotionnels <a href="#entry-Udeviizataka" class="skt-link">Devīśataka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanandin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānandin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aanand" class="skt-link">ānand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanandin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanandinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānandinī</i> qui se réjouit; joyeux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réjouissant.<br> <a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanantarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनन्तर्य</span> <i>ānantarya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-anantara" class="skt-link">anantara</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aanantarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> contiguïté, fait d'être adjacent ou successeur immédiat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proximité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanantya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनन्त्य</span> <i>ānantya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ananta" class="skt-link">ananta</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanantya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanantya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanantyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānantyā</i> infini; éternel — <a href="#entry-aanantya" class="skt-link">n.</a> infinité, infinitude; éternité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> immortalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनर्त</span> <i>ānarta</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aan_rt" class="skt-link">ānṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-aanarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> salle de danse, académie de danse; théâtre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Ānarta, fils de <a href="#entry-Uzaryaati" class="skt-link">Śaryāti</a>; <a href="#entry-Urevata" class="skt-link">Revata</a> est son fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du pays <a href="#entry-Uaanarta" class="skt-link">Ānarta</a>, au <a href="#entry-Ugurjara" class="skt-link">Gurjara</a> (nord du mod. Gujarāt); les <a href="#entry-yaadavaas" class="skt-link">yādavās</a> y régnaient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. les <i>ānartās</i>, ses habitants; ses rois — <a href="#entry-aanarta" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'empire des <a href="#entry-aanartaas" class="skt-link">ānartās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la danse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanartapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānartapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-aanartapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. d'Ānartapura «Capitale d'<a href="#entry-Uaanarta" class="skt-link">Ānarta</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udvaaravatii" class="skt-link">Dvāravatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aanarta" class="skt-link">ānarta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनत</span> <i>ānata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aanam" class="skt-link">ānam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānatā</i> courbé, incliné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soumis, humble, obéissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> salué avec révérence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aandhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन्ध्र</span> <i>āndhra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-andhra" class="skt-link">andhra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aandhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aandhra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aandhraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āndhrā</i> relatif au peuple <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aandhradeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āndhradeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a>] <a href="#entry-aandhradeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. le pays <a href="#entry-andhra" class="skt-link">andhra</a> (mod. <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a> <a href="#entry-pradeza" class="skt-link">pradeśa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aandhrazabda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āndhraśabda</i> [<a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>] <a href="#entry-aandhrazabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> télugu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aandhrazabdacintaama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āndhraśabdacintāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-cintaama_ni" class="skt-link">cintāmaṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-aandhrazabdacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Ādhraśabdacintāmaṇi, grammaire du télugu en sanskrit.<br> <a href="#entry-aandhrazabda" class="skt-link">āndhraśabda</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aandhra" class="skt-link">āndhra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aandola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन्दोल</span> <i>āndola</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-dola" class="skt-link">dola</a>] <a href="#entry-aandola" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de balancer; fait d'éventer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> balançoire — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(āndolayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āndolita</i><i>)</i> balancer; agiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनी</span> <i>ānī</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ānayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ānīta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>ānīya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> amener, apporter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaniila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनील</span> <i>ānīla</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaniila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaniila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaniilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānīlā</i> bleuâtre; noirâtre — <a href="#entry-aaniila" class="skt-link">m.</a> améthyste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaniita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनीत</span> <i>ānīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aanii" class="skt-link">ānī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaniita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaniita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaniitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānītā</i> apporté; amené.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनीय</span> <i>ānīya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-aanii" class="skt-link">ānī</a>] ind. ayant apporté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनिल</span> <i>ānila</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-anila" class="skt-link">anila</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanilii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānilī</i> produit par le vent; venteux — <a href="#entry-aanila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> (fils du vent).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन्तर</span> <i>āntara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-antara" class="skt-link">antara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aantara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aantaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āntarā</i> intérieur; interne; à l'intérieur de; intermédiaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indigène — <a href="#entry-aantara" class="skt-link">m.</a> ami intime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aantarik_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन्तरिक्ष</span> <i>āntarikṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-antarik_sa" class="skt-link">antarikṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aantarik_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aantarik_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aantarik_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āntarikṣī</i> atmosphérique — <a href="#entry-aantarik_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> pluie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanu_sak">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनुषक्</span> <i>ānuṣak</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-anu_sa_nj" class="skt-link">anuṣañj</a>] adv. sans cesse, sans interruption; l'un après l'autre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanuk">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनुक्</span> <i>ānuk</i> nota. accrément du suffixe <a href="#entry--aanii" class="skt-link">-ānī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanupuurva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आनुपूर्व</span> <i>ānupūrva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-anupuurva" class="skt-link">anupūrva</a>] <a href="#entry-aanupuurva" class="skt-link">n.</a> ordre, série, succession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. ordre de précédence des castes.<br> <i>ānupūrveṇa</i> l'un après l'autre, dans l'ordre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanupuurvya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ānupūrvya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aanupuurvya" class="skt-link">n.</a> ordre, succession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. ordre successif des mots dans la phrase ou dans un mot composé.<br> <i>ānupūrvyāt</i> dans l'ordre.<br> <i>ānupūrvyeṇa</i> selon l'ordre.<br> <a href="#entry-aanupuurva" class="skt-link">ānupūrva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aanviik_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आन्वीक्षक</span> <i>ānvīkṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-anviik_s" class="skt-link">anvīkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aanviik_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aanviik_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aanviik_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānvīkṣikā</i> qui examine — <a href="#entry-aanviik_sikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānvīkṣikī</i> examen, investigation rationnelle; philosophie; métaphysique — <a href="#entry-aanviik_sakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ānvīkṣakī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्</span> <i>āp</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(āpnoti)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>āpṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(āpa)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(āpat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpta</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(āptum)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āptavya</i>, <i>āptya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>āptvā</i>, <i>-āpya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> atteindre, obtenir; accomplir; acquérir, gagner; soumettre; parvenir à, commettre ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(āpayati)</i> faire atteindre, satisfaire — ps. <i>(āpyate)</i> être atteint; être obtenu — dés. <i>(īpsati)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-iips" class="skt-link">īps</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>apiscor</i>; fr. apte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aap_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपृ</span> <i>āpṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-p_r_2" class="skt-link">pṛ_2</a>] pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aap_rcchya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपृच्छ्य</span> <i>āpṛcchya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aapraz" class="skt-link">āpraś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aap_rcchya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aap_rcchya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aap_rcchyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpṛcchyā</i> sur quoi l'on doit se renseigner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aap_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपॄ</span> <i>āpṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-p_rr" class="skt-link">pṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(āpiparti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(āpṛṇāti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(āpṛṇati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpūrta</i><i>)</i> combler, remplir, satisfaire — ps. <i>(āpūryate)</i> être rempli, être satisfait — ca. <i>(āpūrayati)</i> remplir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप</span> <i>āpa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-aapas" class="skt-link">āpas</a>] <a href="#entry-aapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Āpa personnifiant l'Eau, l'un des 8 Trésors [<a href="#entry-vasu" class="skt-link">vasu</a>]; il préside le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Upuurvaa_saa_dhaas" class="skt-link">Pūrvāṣāḍhās</a> (δ du Sagittaire).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपण</span> <i>āpaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-pa_na" class="skt-link">paṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aapa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> marché; boutique; commerce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपा</span> <i>āpā</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-paa_1" class="skt-link">pā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āpibati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpīta_1</i><i>)</i> boire de, sucer, siroter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) être tout ouïe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपात</span> <i>āpāta</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aapat" class="skt-link">āpat</a>] <a href="#entry-aapaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> attaque, avance brusque, apparition soudaine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapaddharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āpaddharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-aapaddharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. loi de temps de détresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. état d'urgence.<br> <a href="#entry-aapad_2" class="skt-link">āpad_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āpadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-aapadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āpadeva, brâhmane marathe du début du 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaanyaayaprakaaza" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsānyāyaprakāśa</a>, traité de <a href="#entry-puurvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">pūrvamīmāṃsā</a> — <a href="#entry-aapadevii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpadevī</i> lit. np. de l'Āpadevī, épith. du <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaanyaayaprakaaza" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsānyāyaprakāśa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aapa" class="skt-link">āpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपन्न</span> <i>āpanna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aapad_1" class="skt-link">āpad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aapanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aapannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpannā</i> tombé dans; affligé, malheureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrivé, survenu; obtenu, acquis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a obtenu, qui a acquis ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपस्</span> <i>āpas</i> [nom. pl. <a href="#entry-ap" class="skt-link">ap</a>] ind. les eaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapastamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपस्तम्ब</span> <i>āpastamba</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-apastamba" class="skt-link">apastamba</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aapastamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapastamba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aapastambaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpastambā</i> lit. relatif à ou composé par <a href="#entry-Uapastamba" class="skt-link">Apastamba</a>; not. l'<a href="#entry-Uaapastamba" class="skt-link">Āpastamba</a> <a href="#entry-zulbasuutra" class="skt-link">śulbasūtra</a>, qui indique les mesures des instruments du sacrifice, not. le poteau sacrificiel [<a href="#entry-yuupa" class="skt-link">yūpa</a>] — <a href="#entry-aapastamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āpastamba, philosophe antique (entre 600 et 300 ant.), auteur notamment de l'<a href="#entry-Uaapastamba" class="skt-link">Āpastamba</a> <a href="#entry-dharmasuutra" class="skt-link">dharmasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपत्</span> <i>āpat</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-pat_1" class="skt-link">pat_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āpatati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpatita</i><i>)</i> se précipiter sur, tomber sur, s'élancer vers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपत्ति</span> <i>āpatti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aapad_1" class="skt-link">āpad_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aapatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> événement, occurrence; obtention d'un état <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> détresse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapek_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपेक्ष्</span> <i>āpekṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-apek_s" class="skt-link">apekṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āpekṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpekṣita</i><i>)</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] être dépendant de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपि</span> <i>āpi</i> [<a href="#entry-aap" class="skt-link">āp</a>] <a href="#entry-aapi" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. ami, allié — a. <a href="#entry-aapi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aapi" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpi</i> ifc. qui arrive, qui entre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपी</span> <i>āpī</i> [<a href="#entry-aapyaa" class="skt-link">āpyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aapii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aapii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpī</i> accru, grossi; gros.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapii_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपीड्</span> <i>āpīḍ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-pii_d" class="skt-link">pīḍ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(āpīḍayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpīḍita</i><i>)</i> presser, écraser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapii_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपीड</span> <i>āpīḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-aapii_d" class="skt-link">āpīḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-aapii_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> compression; écrasement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chapelet attaché en couronne sur la tête comme ornement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapizali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपिशलि</span> <i>āpiśali</i> <a href="#entry-aapizali" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āpiśali, grammairien prédécesseur de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>, auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uaapizalizik_saa" class="skt-link">Āpiśaliśikṣā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapizalizik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āpiśaliśikṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-zik_saa" class="skt-link">śikṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-aapizalizik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phon. np. de l'Āpiśaliśikṣā, traité de phonétique d'<a href="#entry-Uaapizali" class="skt-link">Āpiśali</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aapizali" class="skt-link">āpiśali</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaplu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्लु</span> <i>āplu</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-plu" class="skt-link">plu</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āplavate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpluta</i><i>)</i> mouiller, baigner, inonder; plonger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapravaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्रवास</span> <i>āpravāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aapravas" class="skt-link">āpravas</a>] <a href="#entry-aapravaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> retour à son domicile, au pays; immmigration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्रवस्</span> <i>āpravas</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-pravas" class="skt-link">pravas</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āpravasati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āproṣita</i><i>)</i> revenir (de voyage, de l'étranger, d'exil); retourner chez soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapraz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्रश्</span> <i>āpraś</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-praz" class="skt-link">praś</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(āpṛcchate)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āpṛcchya</i><i>)</i> prendre congé, dire au-revoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saluer (visiteur); invoquer (dieu) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> demander, se renseigner de.<br> <i>āpṛcchasva</i> <i>sahacaram</i> dis au-revoir à ton ami.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्त</span> <i>āpta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aap" class="skt-link">āp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āptā</i> obtenu, atteint, reçu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ayant atteint, ayant reçu; responsable, exact, fidèle, fiable; approprié, vraisemblable, adapté, adéquat; compétent, apte; digne de confiance — <a href="#entry-aapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> proche, intime; personne de confiance, ami <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] voyant, personne douée de perception directe et qui transmet un enseignement — <a href="#entry-aapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> équation mathématique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>aptus</i>; fr. apte, adapté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaptadak_si_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āptadakṣiṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaptadak_si_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaptadak_si_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaptadak_si_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āptadakṣiṇā</i> muni de dons appropriés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaptakaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āptakāma</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaptakaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaptakaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaptakaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āptakāmā</i> qui a satisfait ses désirs, satisfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qui connaît l'identité du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> et de l'<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaptavacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āptavacana</i> [<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] <a href="#entry-aaptavacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. parole digne de foi, un moyen de connaissance légitime [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-aaptopadeza" class="skt-link">āptopadeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्ति</span> <i>āpti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aap" class="skt-link">āp</a>] <a href="#entry-aapti" class="skt-link">f.</a> atteinte, obtention; acquisition, gain; abondance, fortune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> connection, rapport, relation; aptitude, adéquation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>āptayas</i> soc. les 12 invocations; la 1⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ est <i>āpaye</i> <a href="#entry-svaahaa" class="skt-link">svāhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaptopadeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āptopadeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-upadeza" class="skt-link">upadeśa</a>] <a href="#entry-aaptopadeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. témoignage transmis par un locuteur digne de foi, un moyen de connaissance légitime [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-aaptavacana" class="skt-link">āptavacana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aapta" class="skt-link">āpta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapuurta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपूर्त</span> <i>āpūrta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aap_rr" class="skt-link">āpṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aapuurta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aapuurta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aapuurtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āpūrtā</i> satisfait — <a href="#entry-aapuurta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de satisfaire; acte méritoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aapyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्या</span> <i>āpyā</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-pyaa" class="skt-link">pyā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āpyāyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āpyāna</i><i>)</i> grossir, enfler, gonfler; engraisser; forcir, profiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élargir, faire grossir; accroître, renforcer; confirmer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्</span> <i>ār</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] pp. <i>(</i><i>ārta</i><i>)</i> avoir des ennuis, tomber en infortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्ष</span> <i>ārṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aar_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārṣā</i> relatif aux sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> archaïque — <a href="#entry-aar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> texte sacré, <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aar_saprayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārṣaprayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-prayoga" class="skt-link">prayoga</a>] <a href="#entry-aar_saprayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. usage non Paninéen dans un texte védique.<br> <a href="#entry-aar_sa" class="skt-link">ārṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aar_seya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्षेय</span> <i>ārṣeya</i> [<a href="#entry-aar_sa" class="skt-link">ārṣa</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aar_seya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aar_seya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aar_seyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārṣeyā</i> relatif aux sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vénérable, respectable — <a href="#entry-aar_seya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lignée de sages; syn. <a href="#entry-pravara_1" class="skt-link">pravara_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर</span> <i>āra</i> <a href="#entry-aara" class="skt-link">n.</a> laiton; fer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> angle; coin — <a href="#entry-aara" class="skt-link">m.</a> cavité — <a href="#entry-aaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārā</i> poinçon, pic; sonde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरण्य</span> <i>āraṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aara_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aara_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aara_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āraṇyā</i> relatif à la forêt, sylvestre; sauvage — <a href="#entry-aara_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>āraṇyās</i> bêtes sauvages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aara_nyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āraṇyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aara_nyikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āraṇyikā</i> de la forêt, sauvage — <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> habitant de la forêt, ermite — <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. sorte de textes brahmaniques ésotériques [«à lire en secret dans la solitude de la forêt»]; cf. <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">Bṛhadāraṇyaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Utaittiriiyaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">Taittirīyāraṇyaka</a>.<br> <i>āraṇyakaṃ</i> <i>parva</i> lit. «le livre de la forêt», 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mahābhārata</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aara_nya" class="skt-link">āraṇya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराध्</span> <i>ārādh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-raadh" class="skt-link">rādh</a>] ca. <i>(ārādhayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ārādhita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ārādhya</i>, <i>ārādhanīya</i><i>)</i> satisfaire, gagner, se concilier; solliciter; honorer, vénérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mériter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराधन</span> <i>ārādhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aaraadh" class="skt-link">ārādh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaraadhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārādhanā</i> qui se rend favorable — <a href="#entry-aaraadhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> hommage, service; fait de gagner qqn. à sa cause <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. adoration d'une idole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mise en œuvre; obtention — f. cf. <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराधना</span> <i>ārādhanā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-aaraadhana" class="skt-link">ārādhana</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jn. tradition jn. d'accomplissement des devoirs religieux, not. du choix de la fin de vie; texte doctrinaire traitant de la fin de vie et des modes de suicide; cf. <a href="#entry-Umuulaaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">Mūlārādhanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhanaakathaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārādhanākathā</i> [<a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaakathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. histoire édifiante sur l'approche de la mort dans la tradition jn.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhanaakathaakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārādhanākathākośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaakathaakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. recueil de telles histoires; cf. <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaakoza" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathākośa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaakathaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanākathā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराधनीय</span> <i>ārādhanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-aaraadh" class="skt-link">ārādh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaraadhaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārādhanīyā</i> dont il faut gagner la faveur; qu'il faut honorer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराधित</span> <i>ārādhita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-aaraadh" class="skt-link">ārādh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaraadhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārādhitā</i> concilié; sollicité; honoré, révéré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accompli, effectué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraadhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराध्य</span> <i>ārādhya</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aaraadh" class="skt-link">ārādh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaraadhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārādhyā</i> dont il faut ou dont on peut gagner la faveur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à honorer; à accomplir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आराम</span> <i>ārāma</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aaram" class="skt-link">āram</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraama" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir, délice; repos <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> endroit d'agrément, lieu de repos; jardin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरात्</span> <i>ārāt</i> var. <i>ārād</i> adv. de loin; loin de ⟨acc. abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraatrika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरात्रिक</span> <i>ārātrika</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-raatrika" class="skt-link">rātrika</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraatrika" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie nocturne de salut d'une divinité par rotation d'une flamme, not. de camphre [<a href="#entry-karpuura" class="skt-link">karpūra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>ārati</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaraavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-aavalii" class="skt-link">āvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-aaraavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. [«rangée de pics»] np. de la chaîne de montagne Ārāvalī au Rajasthan, entre le Mont Abū [<a href="#entry-Uarbudaara_nya" class="skt-link">Arbudāraṇya</a>] et Delhi.<br> <a href="#entry-aara" class="skt-link">āra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarabdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरब्ध</span> <i>ārabdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aarabh" class="skt-link">ārabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarabdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarabdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarabdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārabdhā</i> entrepris; commencé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरभ्</span> <i>ārabh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-rabh" class="skt-link">rabh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(ārabhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ārabdha</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ārabhya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> tenir ferme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entreprendre; commencer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarabha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरभट</span> <i>ārabhaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-aarabh" class="skt-link">ārabh</a>] <a href="#entry-aarabha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> entrepreneur; homme courageux — <a href="#entry-aarabha_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārabhaṭī</i> intrépidité; héroïsme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. représentation de faits héroïques, violents ou surnaturels au théâtre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarabhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरभ्य</span> <i>ārabhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aarabh" class="skt-link">ārabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarabhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarabhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarabhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārabhyā</i> à entreprendre — ind. ayant été commencé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à partir de ⟨abl. acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaragvadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरग्वध</span> <i>āragvadha</i> <a href="#entry-aaragvadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cassia fistula</i>, arbre aux feuilles composées, aux jolies fleurs jaunes en grappes pendantes, utilisé en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-aruja" class="skt-link">aruja</a>, <a href="#entry-k_rtamaala" class="skt-link">kṛtamāla</a>, <a href="#entry-ja_tharanud" class="skt-link">jaṭharanud</a> — <a href="#entry-aaragvadha" class="skt-link">n.</a> son fruit, une gousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरम्</span> <i>āram</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āramati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ārata</i><i>)</i> s'arrêter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se réjouir, se plaire; prendre plaisir à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरम्भ</span> <i>ārambha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aarabh" class="skt-link">ārabh</a>] <a href="#entry-aarambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> entreprise; commencement, origine; hâte, effort.<br> <i>ārambhaḥ</i> <i>kadā</i> <i>bhavati</i> quand est-ce que ça commence?<br> <i>kimāraṃbhastvam</i> qu'est ce que tu fais?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarambhopaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārambhopāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">upāya</a>] <a href="#entry-aarambhopaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> initialisation.<br> <a href="#entry-aarambha" class="skt-link">ārambha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarbhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्भव</span> <i>ārbhava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_rbhu" class="skt-link">ṛbhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarbhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarbhava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarbhavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārbhavī</i> véd. concernant les <a href="#entry-_rbhavas" class="skt-link">ṛbhavas</a>; sacré pour les <a href="#entry-_rbhavas" class="skt-link">ṛbhavas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aardhadhaatuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
