    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्धधातुक</span> <i>ārdhadhātuka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ardhadhaatu" class="skt-link">ardhadhātu</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aardhadhaatuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aardhadhaatuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aardhadhaatukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārdhadhātukā</i> gram. [«applicable à une forme plus courte de la base verbale»] se dit des finales du parfait et du bénédictif, et de certains suffixes [<a href="#entry-pratyaya" class="skt-link">pratyaya</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-saarvadhaatuka" class="skt-link">sārvadhātuka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aardra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्द्र</span> <i>ārdra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ard" class="skt-link">ard</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aardra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aardra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aardraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārdrā</i> mouillé, humide; frais, vert, nouveau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tendre, doux, sensible; mou, flasque — n. cf. <a href="#entry-aardraka" class="skt-link">ārdraka</a> — <a href="#entry-aardraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārdrā</i> astr. np. d'Ārdrā «la Fraîche», 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile Bételgeuse; <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> y préside; son symbole est une pierre précieuse; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubaahuu" class="skt-link">Bāhū</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aardraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārdraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-aardraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Zingiber officinale</i>, gingembre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. sa racine, utilisée comme condiment à l'état frais (opp. <a href="#entry-zu_n_thii" class="skt-link">śuṇṭhī</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>adrak</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aardravaasas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārdravāsas</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasas" class="skt-link">vāsas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aardravaasas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aardravaasas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aardravaasas" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a ses vêtements mouillés.<br> <a href="#entry-aardra" class="skt-link">ārdra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarjava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्जव</span> <i>ārjava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_rj" class="skt-link">ṛj</a><a href="#entry--va" class="skt-link">-va</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarjava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarjava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarjavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārjavā</i> honnête, sincère — <a href="#entry-aarjava" class="skt-link">n.</a> droiture, rectitude, sincérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarjiika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्जीक</span> <i>ārjīka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_rj" class="skt-link">ṛj</a><a href="#entry--iika" class="skt-link">-īka</a>] <a href="#entry-aarjiika" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. région riche en <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>; récipient à <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarogya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोग्य</span> <i>ārogya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aroga" class="skt-link">aroga</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aarogya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bonne santé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaroh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोह्</span> <i>āroh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(ārohayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ārohayate)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-aarop" class="skt-link">ārop</a>.<br> <i>kareṇur</i> <i>ārohayate</i> <i>niṣādinam</i> L'éléphant se laisse monter par son conducteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaroha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोह</span> <i>āroha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] <a href="#entry-aaroha" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui est monté sur, cavalier — <a href="#entry-aaroha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascension; orgueil; altitude, éminence — <a href="#entry-aaroha" class="skt-link">m.</a> croupe, hanches de la femme (servant de siège à son jeune enfant).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaroha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोहण</span> <i>ārohaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] <a href="#entry-aaroha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> ascension.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarohin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोहिन्</span> <i>ārohin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarohin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarohin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarohi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārohiṇī</i> qui grimpe, qui monte; not. cavalier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarop">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोप्</span> <i>ārop</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(āropayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āropita</i><i>)</i> lever, dresser; faire monter sur ⟨acc.⟩; faire pousser; placer (à un poste élevé) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attacher (not. une corde à un arc).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaropa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोप</span> <i>āropa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aarop" class="skt-link">ārop</a>] <a href="#entry-aaropa" class="skt-link">m.</a> imposition; charge, attribution; action de placer en hauteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. surimposition [<a href="#entry-upacaara" class="skt-link">upacāra</a>] d'une figure [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇā</a>]; se distingue de l'absorption [<a href="#entry-adhyavasaana" class="skt-link">adhyavasāna</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaropa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोपण</span> <i>āropaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aarop" class="skt-link">ārop</a>] <a href="#entry-aaropa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte de faire monter sur (not. le bûcher funéraire) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> action de lever, de dresser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> action d'équiper un arc avec sa corde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaropita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोपित</span> <i>āropita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aarop" class="skt-link">ārop</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaropita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaropita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaropitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āropitā</i> élevé; placé sur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaropya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरोप्य</span> <i>āropya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aarop" class="skt-link">ārop</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaropya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaropya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaropyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āropyā</i> à placer sur; à faire monter, à dresser; qui doit être élevé — ind. ayant été élevé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्त</span> <i>ārta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aar" class="skt-link">ār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aartaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārtā</i> atteint de, opprimé par ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tombé dans le malheur; affligé; tourmenté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blessé; souffrant, malade; malheureux — <a href="#entry-aartaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārtā</i> état d'affliction; souffrance.<br> <i>ato</i> <i>'nyad</i> <i>ārtam</i> [BU.] Tout autre (état que celui d'absorption dans le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>) est souffrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aartaayani">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्तायनि</span> <i>ārtāyani</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_rtaayana" class="skt-link">ṛtāyana</a>] <a href="#entry-aartaayani" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Ārtāyani, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uzalya" class="skt-link">Śalya</a> «Dont les ancêtres ont suivi la voie juste».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aartataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārtatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-aartataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état d'affliction; souffrance.<br> <a href="#entry-aarta" class="skt-link">ārta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aartava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्तव</span> <i>ārtava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-_rtu" class="skt-link">ṛtu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aartava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aartava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aartavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārtavī</i> saisonnier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif aux menstruations — <a href="#entry-aartava" class="skt-link">m.</a> partie de l'année — <a href="#entry-aartava" class="skt-link">n.</a> menstruations — <a href="#entry-aartavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārtavī</i> femme pendant ses règles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jument.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्थ</span> <i>ārtha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aarthii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārthī</i> concernant une chose ou un objet; matériel, concret; relatif à une possession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. relatif au sens, sémantique — <a href="#entry-aartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. le signifié (opp. <a href="#entry-zaabda" class="skt-link">śābda</a> le signifiant).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्ति</span> <i>ārti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aar" class="skt-link">ār</a>] <a href="#entry-aarti" class="skt-link">f.</a> peine, souffrance (physique ou morale).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aartinaazana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ārtināśana</i> [<a href="#entry-naazana" class="skt-link">nāśana</a>] <a href="#entry-aartinaazana" class="skt-link">n.</a> destruction de la souffrance.<br> <a href="#entry-aarti" class="skt-link">ārti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaru_neya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āruṇeya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-aaru_neya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'<a href="#entry-aaru_neya" class="skt-link">āruṇeya</a> <a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [BU.] np. d'Āruṇeya, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uzvetaketu" class="skt-link">Śvetaketu</a>, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uuddaalaka" class="skt-link">Uddālaka</a> <a href="#entry-Uaaru_ni" class="skt-link">Āruṇi</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aaru_ni" class="skt-link">āruṇi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaru_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुणि</span> <i>āruṇi</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aru_ni" class="skt-link">aruṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-aaru_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [ChU.] patr. d'<a href="#entry-Uuddaalaka" class="skt-link">Uddālaka</a> Āruṇi, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaru_ni" class="skt-link">Aruṇi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaru_sii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुषी</span> <i>āruṣī</i> <a href="#entry-aaru_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. d'Āruṣī «Furie», fille de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ucyavana" class="skt-link">Cyavana</a>; persécutée par les <a href="#entry-k_satriyaas" class="skt-link">kṣatriyās</a>, elle se réfugia enceinte dans une cave, où elle accoucha d'<a href="#entry-Uaurva" class="skt-link">Aurva</a> par la cuisse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaruh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुह्</span> <i>āruh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ārohati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ārūḍha</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>āruhya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>adhi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> monter à, sur ⟨acc.⟩; grimper à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'élever à, se placer sur, faire l'ascension de ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(ārohayati)</i> var. <i>āropayati</i> lever, dresser; faire monter sur ⟨acc.⟩; faire pousser; placer (à un poste élevé) — ca. md. <i>(ārohayate)</i> être monté par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attacher (not. une corde à un arc).<br> <i>āruha</i> <i>mama</i> <i>śroṇīṃ</i> <i>neṣyāmi</i> <i>tvāṃ</i> <i>vihāyasā</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Grimpe sur ma hanche, je te conduirai dans les airs (ogresse).<br> <i>vṛkṣam</i> <i>āruh</i> monter à un arbre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaruhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुह्य</span> <i>āruhya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] ind. ayant surmonté, ayant atteint.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaruj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुज्</span> <i>āruj</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-ruj_1" class="skt-link">ruj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ārujati)</i> briser, démolir; interrompre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaruka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरुक</span> <i>āruka</i> <a href="#entry-aaruka" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Prunus domestica</i>, prune; le prunier est cultivé en altitude, dans les Monts Bleus [<a href="#entry-Uniilagiri_1" class="skt-link">Nīlagiri_1</a>] et dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaruu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आरूढ</span> <i>ārūḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aaruh" class="skt-link">āruh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaruu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaruu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaruu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ārūḍhā</i> qui est monté, qui a grimpé sur; qui a réussi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> atteint, escaladé — ifc. ayant pour monture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्य</span> <i>ārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryā</i> [«aux aspirations élevées»] noble, généreux; respectable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> noble, de race aryenne, bien né; se dit du peuple <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>, qui émigra d'Asie centrale en <a href="#entry-aaryaavarta" class="skt-link">āryāvarta</a>; il est issu de l'ancêtre mythique <a href="#entry-Upuru" class="skt-link">Puru</a> — <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître, chef de maison; honnête homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adr. Votre Excellence, Noble, Honorable — <a href="#entry-aaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryā</i> noble dame <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Āryā, l'une des 7 «mères (dangereuses) aux enfants» [<a href="#entry-zizumaataras" class="skt-link">śiśumātaras</a>] associées à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. nom d'un mètre de 30+27 temps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prakrit ayya,ajja; pali <i>ariya</i>; fr. aryen; celte erinn; perse Iran; tamil Iyer; telugu Iyengar.<br> <i>kim</i> <i>atra</i> <i>āryasya</i> (poli) que puis-je faire pour Son Excellence? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (brusque) que faites vous ici?<br> <i>āryā</i> <i>vāk</i> f. sanskrit védique.<br> <i>kṛṇvantu</i> <i>viśvam</i> <i>āryam</i> [RV.] Rendez ce monde noble (devise de l'<a href="#entry-Uaaryasamaaja" class="skt-link">Āryasamāja</a>).<br> <i>jāto</i> <i>nāryām</i> <i>anāryāyām</i> <i>āryād</i> <i>āryo</i> <i>bhaved</i> <i>guṇaiḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>anāryāj</i> <i>jāta</i> <i>āryāyām</i> <i>anārya</i> <i>iti</i> <i>niścaya</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [Manu] Une personne née d'un homme noble [<a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>] par une femme non-noble peut devenir noble par ses vertus, mais une personne née d'un homme non-noble n'est certes pas noble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्या</span> <i>āryā</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaa_s_taafgamaarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryāṣṭāṅgamārga</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_taafgamaarga" class="skt-link">aṣṭāṅgamārga</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaa_s_taafgamaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. cf. <a href="#entry-aaryamaarga" class="skt-link">āryamārga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaa_s_tazata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryāṣṭaśata</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_tazata" class="skt-link">aṣṭaśata</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaa_s_tazata" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āryāṣṭaśata, section de l'<a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_tiiya" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭīya</a> de 108 vers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaasamaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryāsamāja</i> [<a href="#entry-samaaja" class="skt-link">samāja</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaasamaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> mod. np. du mouvement de renouveau hindou Āryāsamāja «Assemblée des Purs» ou Āryasamāja «Noble assemblée», fondé par <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> <a href="#entry-Udayaananda_1" class="skt-link">Dayānanda_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a> en 1875; il interdit not. le culte des idoles [<a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a>] et prône le retour aux Védas considérés comme révélation divine.<br> <a href="#entry-aaryaa" class="skt-link">āryā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaasaptazatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryāsaptaśatī</i> [<a href="#entry-saptazatii" class="skt-link">saptaśatī</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaasaptazatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du poème lyrique Āryāsaptaśatī «700 vers en mètre noble», dû à <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana_1" class="skt-link">Govardhana_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaavarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryāvarta</i> [<a href="#entry-aavarta" class="skt-link">āvarta</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaavarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. [«terre des aryens»] véd. np. de l'Āryāvarta ou plaine indo-gangétique.<br> <i>ā</i> <i>samudrāt</i> <i>tu</i> <i>vai</i> <i>pūrvād</i> <i>ā</i> <i>samudrāc</i> <i>ca</i> <i>paścimāt</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>tayor</i> <i>evāntaram</i> <i>giryor</i> <i>āryāvartam</i> <i>vidur</i> <i>budhāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [MS.] Les sages considèrent que l'<a href="#entry-Uaaryaavarta" class="skt-link">Āryāvarta</a> s'étend de la mer de l'Est à la mer de l'Ouest jusqu'aux montagnes entre les deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryabha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryabhaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āryabhaṭa, astronome et mathématicien originaire du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a> (476–~550); il enseignait à <a href="#entry-Ukusumapura" class="skt-link">Kusumapura</a>; il étudia les fonctions trigonométriques [<a href="#entry-jyaa_3" class="skt-link">jyā_3</a>], calcula π comme 3 . 1416, et évalua l'année solaire à 365,3586805 jours; il proposa une théorie héliocentrique de l'Univers, avec une Terre sphérique tournant sur son axe, dont il mesura la circonférence; on lui doit le traité <a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_tiiya" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭīya</a>; les arabes l'appelent Arjabhad, les latins Ardubarius; le 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ satellite indien (1975) fut nommé en son honneur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryabha_tiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryabhaṭīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryabha_tiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Āryabhaṭīya, ouvrage d'astronomie et de mathématiques dû à <a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭa</a> (499); il comprend 2 parties: la <a href="#entry-Udazagiitikaa" class="skt-link">Daśagītikā</a> et l'<a href="#entry-Uaaryaa_s_tazata" class="skt-link">Āryāṣṭaśata</a>; il fut traduit en latin au 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.<br> <a href="#entry-aaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">āryabhaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryadaasii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryadāsī</i> [<a href="#entry-daasii" class="skt-link">dāsī</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryadaasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. de la fille de ferme [<a href="#entry-gopakanyaa" class="skt-link">gopakanyā</a>] Āryadāsī «Servante de noble», incarnation précédente de <a href="#entry-Utaaraavalii" class="skt-link">Tārāvalī</a>; elle fut amante de <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka_2" class="skt-link">Śaunaka_2</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uzuudraka_2" class="skt-link">Śūdraka_2</a>; elle conçut de ce dernier un garçon qui fut abandonné, recueilli par <a href="#entry-Uvinayavatii" class="skt-link">Vinayavatī</a>, et qui renaquit comme <a href="#entry-Uarthapaala" class="skt-link">Arthapāla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āryadeva, philosophe bd. disciple de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a> qui l'intronisa comme son successeur (vers 250); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ucatu_hzataka" class="skt-link">Catuḥśataka</a>, du Hastavālaprakaraṇa et du <a href="#entry-Ucaryaamelaapakapradiipa" class="skt-link">Caryāmelāpakapradīpa</a> — <a href="#entry-aaryadevii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryadevī</i> hist. np. d'Āryadevī, mère de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; elle vivait à Veliyanad, près de mod. Kochi au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>; elle est aussi appelée Āryambā.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आर्यक</span> <i>āryaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaryaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaryaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaryakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryakā</i> <a href="#entry-aaryikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryikā</i> noble, honorable, respectable — <a href="#entry-aaryaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand-père, aïeul <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> Āryaka «Aïeul», prince <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> de la lignée d'<a href="#entry-Uairaavata" class="skt-link">Airāvata</a>; <a href="#entry-Ucikura" class="skt-link">Cikura</a> est son fils; à la demande de <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a>, il donna son petit-fils <a href="#entry-Usumukha" class="skt-link">Sumukha</a> en mariage à sa fille <a href="#entry-Ugu_nakezii" class="skt-link">Guṇakeśī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. d'Āryaka_1, fils de bouvier, noble caractère dans la pièce <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>; emprisonné par le roi <a href="#entry-Upaalaka" class="skt-link">Pālaka</a>, il s'échappe grâce à <a href="#entry-Uzarvilaka" class="skt-link">Śarvilaka</a> et le détrône — <a href="#entry-aaryaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie en honneur des mânes [<a href="#entry-pit_r" class="skt-link">pitṛ</a>]; récipient utilisé pour ce rite — <a href="#entry-aaryikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryikā</i> jn. nonne nomade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryamaarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryamārga</i> [<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryamaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. «Noble Chemin» menant à la cessation de la souffrance selon l'enseignement du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_taafgamaarga" class="skt-link">aṣṭāṅgamārga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryapraaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryaprāya</i> [<a href="#entry-praaya" class="skt-link">prāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaryapraaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaryapraaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaryapraayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āryaprāyā</i> (pays) presqu'entièrement de peuple <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. d'Āryapura au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> (mod. Aihole); le prince <a href="#entry-Ujayasi_mha" class="skt-link">Jayasiṃha</a> en fit la première capitale <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> au 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryaputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryaputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryaputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. «fils de noble race», titre honorifique donné par une femme à son époux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryasatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryasatya</i> [<a href="#entry-satya" class="skt-link">satya</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryasatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. «Noble Vérité», proclamée par <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; les Quatre Nobles Vérités [<i>catvāryāryasatyāni</i>] sont: la vérité de la souffrance [<a href="#entry-du_hkha" class="skt-link">duḥkha</a>], la vérité de l'origine de la souffrance [<a href="#entry-samudaaya" class="skt-link">samudāya</a>], la vérité de la cessation de la souffrance [<a href="#entry-nirodha" class="skt-link">nirodha</a>] et la vérité du noble chemin [<a href="#entry-aaryamaarga" class="skt-link">āryamārga</a>] menant à la cessation de la souffrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaryasuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āryasūra</i> [<a href="#entry-suura" class="skt-link">sūra</a>] <a href="#entry-aaryasuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āryasūra, auteur du premier recueil de la <a href="#entry-Ujaatakamaalaa" class="skt-link">Jātakamālā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzuura_1" class="skt-link">Śūra_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aas_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसृ</span> <i>āsṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-s_r" class="skt-link">sṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āsarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsṛta</i><i>)</i> se hâter vers, arriver en courant; tomber à verse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasaad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसाद्</span> <i>āsād</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(āsādayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsādita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āsādya</i>, <i>āsādayitavya</i><i>)</i> faire asseoir; poser, placer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rencontrer; effectuer; atteindre, obtenir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसाम्</span> <i>āsām</i> [g. pl. <a href="#entry-iyam" class="skt-link">iyam</a>] ind. de ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसार</span> <i>āsāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aas_r" class="skt-link">āsṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aasaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> attaque; incursion; encerclement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> averse, forte pluie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasaarazarkaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsāraśarkarā</i> [<a href="#entry-zarkaraa" class="skt-link">śarkarā</a>] <a href="#entry-aasaarazarkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pl. averse de grêle.<br> <a href="#entry-aasaara" class="skt-link">āsāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसद्</span> <i>āsad</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sad_1" class="skt-link">sad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āsīdati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsanna</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>āsādya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'asseoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> présider; attendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> atteindre, approcher, trouver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entreprendre, commencer — ca. <i>(āsādayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsadita</i><i>)</i> trouver, rencontrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasadana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसदन</span> <i>āsadana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] <a href="#entry-aasadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de s'asseoir; fait d'atteindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> siège.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasadita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसदित</span> <i>āsadita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasadita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasadita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasaditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsaditā</i> posé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> atteint.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसङ्ग</span> <i>āsaṅga</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasa_nj" class="skt-link">āsañj</a>] <a href="#entry-aasafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> attachement; contact, association; dévotion — a. <a href="#entry-aasafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsaṅgā</i> ininterrompu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसक्त</span> <i>āsakta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aasa_nj" class="skt-link">āsañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsaktā</i> attaché, appliqué à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसक्ति</span> <i>āsakti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasa_nj" class="skt-link">āsañj</a>] <a href="#entry-aasakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> attachement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसम्</span> <i>āsam</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-as_2" class="skt-link">as_2</a>] ifc. ind. ayant jeté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasamudram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसमुद्रम्</span> <i>āsamudram</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-samudra_1" class="skt-link">samudra_1</a>] adv. jusqu'à la mer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसन्</span> <i>āsan</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-aasya" class="skt-link">āsya</a>] <a href="#entry-aasan" class="skt-link">n.</a> bouche, visage, gueule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसन</span> <i>āsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> siège; poste, situation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> posture, manière d'être assis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. posture rituelle de <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-kukku_taasana" class="skt-link">kukkuṭāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-gomukhaasana" class="skt-link">gomukhāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-dhanuraasana" class="skt-link">dhanurāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-padmaasana" class="skt-link">padmāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-pazcimaataanaasana" class="skt-link">paścimātānāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-bhadraasana" class="skt-link">bhadrāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-mayuuraasana" class="skt-link">mayūrāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-raajaliilaasana" class="skt-link">rājalīlāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-lalitaasana" class="skt-link">lalitāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-vajraasana" class="skt-link">vajrāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-viiraasana" class="skt-link">vīrāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-zavaasana" class="skt-link">śavāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-sarvaafgaasana" class="skt-link">sarvāṅgāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-si_mhaasana" class="skt-link">siṃhāsana</a>, <a href="#entry-svastikaasana" class="skt-link">svastikāsana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séance, session; tribunal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le contrôle de son corps, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ étape du <a href="#entry-raajayoga" class="skt-link">rājayoga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_taafgayoga" class="skt-link">aṣṭāṅgayoga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasandii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसन्दी</span> <i>āsandī</i> [<a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] <a href="#entry-aasandii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. trône d'osier utilisé dans les rites véd. solennels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसन्न</span> <i>āsanna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsannā</i> assis, posé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proche, voisin, voisin de; prochain — <a href="#entry-aasanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> imminence.<br> <i>āsanne</i> adv. dans le voisinage, à proximité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasannakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsannakāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aasannakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> l'heure de la mort — a. <a href="#entry-aasannakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasannakaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasannakaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsannakālā</i> qui a atteint sa dernière heure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasannatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsannatara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasannatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasannatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasannataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsannatarā</i> compar. plus proche.<br> <a href="#entry-aasanna" class="skt-link">āsanna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसत्ति</span> <i>āsatti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] <a href="#entry-aasatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> vicinité, proximité; union intime; connexion, lien immédiat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. accord entre mots <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perplexité; situation sans issue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasattiyoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsattiyoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasattiyoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasattiyoga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasattiyogaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsattiyogā</i> intimement joint.<br> <a href="#entry-aasatti" class="skt-link">āsatti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसव</span> <i>āsava</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasu_2" class="skt-link">āsu_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aasava" class="skt-link">m.</a> décoction, fermentation; distillation — <a href="#entry-aasava" class="skt-link">m.</a> jus, nectar; boisson fermentée ou distillée; rhum.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasa_nj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसञ्ज्</span> <i>āsañj</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sa_nj" class="skt-link">sañj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āsajati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsakta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> attacher, pendre; s'attacher à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasecana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसेचन</span> <i>āsecana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasic" class="skt-link">āsic</a>] <a href="#entry-aasecana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cavité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasecanavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsecanavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasecanavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasecanavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasecanavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsecanavatī</i> qui a une cavité; creux.<br> <a href="#entry-aasecana" class="skt-link">āsecana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasev">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसेव्</span> <i>āsev</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sev" class="skt-link">sev</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āsevate)</i> fréquenter; habiter; servir, honorer; prendre le parti de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apprécier; aimer; jouir de; pratiquer, s'adonner à (not. sexe).<br> <i>vātam</i> <i>āsev</i> prendre l'air.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasic">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसिच्</span> <i>āsic</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sic" class="skt-link">sic</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(āsiñcati)</i> verser dans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasidhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसिधार</span> <i>āsidhāra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-asidhaaraa" class="skt-link">asidhārā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasidhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasidhaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasidhaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsidhārā</i> comme le fil d'une épée; se dit not. d'un vœu difficile [<a href="#entry-asidhaaraavrata" class="skt-link">asidhārāvrata</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसीन</span> <i>āsīna</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasiinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsīnā</i> assis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसित</span> <i>āsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsitā</i> assis; resté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaspada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्पद</span> <i>āspada</i> [<a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>-<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-aaspada" class="skt-link">n.</a> lieu, place, séjour, siège; occasion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasphal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्फल्</span> <i>āsphal</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sphal" class="skt-link">sphal</a>] ca. <i>(āsphalayati)</i> frapper, faire vibrer; battre, fouetter; déchirer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jouer (de la <a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasraava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्राव</span> <i>āsrāva</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aasru" class="skt-link">āsru</a>] <a href="#entry-aasraava" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui suinte — <a href="#entry-aasraava" class="skt-link">m.</a> blessure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasrava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्रव</span> <i>āsrava</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aasru" class="skt-link">āsru</a>] <a href="#entry-aasrava" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. attraction de l'âme par les objets du monde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्रु</span> <i>āsru</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-sru" class="skt-link">sru</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āsravati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsruta</i><i>)</i> couler de, s'écouler de, suinter de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fuir (d'un récipient).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aast_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्तॄ</span> <i>āstṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-st_rr" class="skt-link">stṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(āstṛṇoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(āstṛṇute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āstīrṇa</i><i>)</i> étendre, étaler, épandre; joncher, couvrir de ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्तर</span> <i>āstara</i> [<a href="#entry-aast_rr" class="skt-link">āstṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-aastara" class="skt-link">n.</a> couverture; tapis; coussin; lit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āstaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aastara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'étendre, d'étaler (not. un tapis, un tissu); fait de joncher (de fleurs) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couverture; coussin; lit; jonchée — <a href="#entry-aastara_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āstaraṇī</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-aastara" class="skt-link">āstara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्थान</span> <i>āsthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aasthaa_2" class="skt-link">āsthā_2</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-aasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> endroit, site, base <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assemblée; salle d'audience — <a href="#entry-aasthaanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsthānī</i> assemblée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्थेय</span> <i>āstheya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-aasthaa_1" class="skt-link">āsthā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aastheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aastheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aastheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āstheyā</i> dont on doit approcher ou se saisir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à pratiquer; qui doit être considéré comme ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui doit être reconnu (opinion).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasthita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्थित</span> <i>āsthita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aasthaa_1" class="skt-link">āsthā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasthita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasthita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasthitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsthitā</i> qui habite, qui réside <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tombé dans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> existant; croyant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habité, occupé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entrepris, accompli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्तीर्ण</span> <i>āstīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aast_rr" class="skt-link">āstṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aastiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aastiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aastiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āstīrṇā</i> étalé, éparpillé, jonché <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couvert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्तिक</span> <i>āstika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-asti" class="skt-link">asti</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aastika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aastika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aastikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āstikī</i> [«qui croit en l'existence (de Dieu ou d'un principe suprême transcendant le réel)»] croyant, pieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qui reconnaît l'autorité du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-naastika" class="skt-link">nāstika</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'Āstika «le Pieux», fils de <a href="#entry-Ujaratkaaru" class="skt-link">Jaratkāru</a> et de <a href="#entry-Umanasaa" class="skt-link">Manasā</a>; il interrompit le sacrifice de serpents de <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aastikya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āstikya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aastikya" class="skt-link">n.</a> foi; piété.<br> <a href="#entry-aastika" class="skt-link">āstika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आसुर</span> <i>āsura</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsurī</i> spirituel, divin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif aux démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>], démoniaque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. forme de mariage où le marié achète sa femme à ses parents — <a href="#entry-aasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsurī</i> démon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. astr. étoiles de l'hémisphère Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्वाद</span> <i>āsvāda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aasvad" class="skt-link">āsvad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvaada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvaadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsvādā</i> qui goûte — <a href="#entry-aasvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> ingestion; jouissance; goût, attrait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasvaadita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्वादित</span> <i>āsvādita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aasvad" class="skt-link">āsvad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasvaadita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvaadita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvaaditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsvāditā</i> goûté, apprécié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasvad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्वद्</span> <i>āsvad</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-svad" class="skt-link">svad</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āsvadati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āsvādita</i><i>)</i> manger — ca. <i>(āsvādayati)</i> goûter; jouir de; manger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasvadavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsvadavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aasvadavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvadavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aasvadavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āsvadavatī</i> qui a bon goût, délicieux.<br> <a href="#entry-aasvaada" class="skt-link">āsvāda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्य</span> <i>āsya</i> [<a href="#entry-aas_3" class="skt-link">ās_3</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bouche, visage, gueule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आस्या</span> <i>āsyā</i> [<a href="#entry-aas_2" class="skt-link">ās_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'être assis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> demeure; état de repos.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aasyasava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āsyasava</i> [<a href="#entry-sava_1" class="skt-link">sava_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aasyasava" class="skt-link">m.</a> salive; crachat.<br> <a href="#entry-aasya" class="skt-link">āsya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्</span> <i>āt</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-ayam" class="skt-link">ayam</a>] adv. sur ce, alors; de plus.<br> <i>ād</i> <i>id</i> alors justement; c'est juste à ce moment que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat_r_n_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतृण्ण</span> <i>ātṛṇṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aat_rd" class="skt-link">ātṛd</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aat_r_n_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aat_r_n_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aat_r_n_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātṛṇṇā</i> percé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat_rd">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतृद्</span> <i>ātṛd</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-t_rd" class="skt-link">tṛd</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ātṛṇatti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātṛṇṇa</i><i>)</i> diviser; percer — inf. <i>(ātṛdas)</i> transpercer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat_rp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतृप्</span> <i>ātṛp</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(ātṛpyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātṛpta</i><i>)</i> être satisfait; prendre plaisir à ⟨g. i.⟩; devenir joyeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat_rpya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतृप्य</span> <i>ātṛpya</i> [<a href="#entry-aat_rp" class="skt-link">ātṛp</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aat_rpya" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. [«succulent»] bot. <i>Anonna squamosa</i>, arbre à anone — <a href="#entry-aat_rpya" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. son fruit, l'anone, comestible; syn. <a href="#entry-siitaaphala" class="skt-link">sītāphala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aat_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतॄ</span> <i>ātṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ātarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātīrṇa</i><i>)</i> dompter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aataana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतान</span> <i>ātāna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aatan" class="skt-link">ātan</a>] <a href="#entry-aataana" class="skt-link">m.</a> extension, étirement; corde tendue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतङ्क</span> <i>ātaṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-aata_nc" class="skt-link">ātañc</a>] <a href="#entry-aatafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> maladie; fièvre; appréhension, peur — <a href="#entry-aatafkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātaṅkā</i> ifc. id.<br> <i>sātaṅka</i> effrayé, terrifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatak">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतक्</span> <i>ātak</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-tak" class="skt-link">tak</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ātanakti)</i> coaguler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतन्</span> <i>ātan</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(ātanoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(ātanute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātata</i><i>)</i> étendre, étirer; couvrir, illuminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>extendere</i>; fr. étendre.<br> <i>padaṃ</i> <i>ātan</i> s'étendre, améliorer sa position.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतन</span> <i>ātana</i> [<a href="#entry-aatan" class="skt-link">ātan</a>] <a href="#entry-aatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> extension; expansion, manifestation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> champ visuel; panorama.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतप्</span> <i>ātap</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ātapati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ātapate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ātapita</i><i>)</i> embraser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतप</span> <i>ātapa</i> [<a href="#entry-aatap" class="skt-link">ātap</a>] <a href="#entry-aatapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaleur du soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatapatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātapatra</i> [<a href="#entry-tra" class="skt-link">tra</a>] <a href="#entry-aatapatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«qui protège de la chaleur»] parasol.<br> <a href="#entry-aatapa" class="skt-link">ātapa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतत</span> <i>ātata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aatan" class="skt-link">ātan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātatā</i> étendu, étiré; tendu, tiré (arc, corde).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatataayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आततायिन्</span> <i>ātatāyin</i> [<a href="#entry-aatata" class="skt-link">ātata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatataayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatataayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatataayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātatāyinī</i> qui a l'arc tiré — <a href="#entry-aatataayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«prêt à tuer»] assassin; criminel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aata_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतञ्च्</span> <i>ātañc</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-ta_nc" class="skt-link">tañc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ātanakti)</i> se coaguler; précipiter (liquide).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatitheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतिथेय</span> <i>ātitheya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-atithi" class="skt-link">atithi</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatitheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatitheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatitheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātitheyā</i> hospitalier — <a href="#entry-aatitheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> hospitalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatithya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतिथ्य</span> <i>ātithya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-atithi" class="skt-link">atithi</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatithya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatithya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatithyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātithyā</i> hospitalier — <a href="#entry-aatithya" class="skt-link">n.</a> hospitalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्म</span> <i>ātma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaarpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmārpaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-arpa_na" class="skt-link">arpaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmaarpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. soumission de soi (au Seigneur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaarpa_nastuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmārpaṇastuti</i> [<a href="#entry-stuti" class="skt-link">stuti</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmaarpa_nastuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Ātmārpaṇastuti, poème mystiaue en 50 stances de Appayya <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>, écrit sous l'influence de la datura [<a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">mohana</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-aatmaarpa_na" class="skt-link">ātmārpaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmabandhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmabandhu</i> [<a href="#entry-bandhu" class="skt-link">bandhu</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmabandhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> cousin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmabhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmabhava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmabhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> génération spontanée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmabheda</i> [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> différence essentielle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de l'Ātmabodha «Connaissance de l'Âme», texte non dualiste [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>] attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmacchandaanucaari_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmacchandānucāriṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-chanda" class="skt-link">chanda</a>-<a href="#entry-anucaarin" class="skt-link">anucārin</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmacchandaanucaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> femme de mœurs libres [«qui suit son propre désir»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmad_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmadṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-d_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmad_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. connaissance profonde de l'essence des êtres et des choses; opp. <a href="#entry-carmad_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">carmadṛṣṭi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. croyance en la réalité du Soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmadaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmadāna</i> [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmadaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. don de soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaguptaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaguptā</i> [<a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmaguptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Mucuna pruriens</i>, pois mascate; légumineuse grimpante, elle a des grappes de fleurs pourpres, et les gousses de ses fruits sont couvertes de poils urticants; elle est utilisée comme fourrage; elle est aphrodisiaque, et contient un précurseur de la dopamine; elle est employée en médecine, contre les morsures de serpent, et pour soulager de la maladie de Parkinson [<a href="#entry-kampavaata" class="skt-link">kampavāta</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-kapikacchu" class="skt-link">kapikacchu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmahatyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmahatyā</i> [<a href="#entry-hatyaa" class="skt-link">hatyā</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmahatyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> suicide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmahita</i> [<a href="#entry-hita_2" class="skt-link">hita_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> son propre bien-être.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmajā</i> né de soi-même — <a href="#entry-aatmaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> (son propre) fils — <a href="#entry-aatmajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmajā</i> (sa propre) fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmajñā</i> qui se connaît soi-même <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] qui connaît l'Esprit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्मक</span> <i>ātmaka</i> [<a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmikā</i> qui appartient à, qui forme la nature de ⟨g.⟩ — ifc. qui est de la nature ou du caractère de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui consiste en; composé de.<br> <i>eka</i> <i>śabdātmikā</i> <i>māyā</i> Un est le symbole de l'illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmakaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmakāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara_na" class="skt-link">kāraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmakaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> raison personnelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmamaa_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmamāṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmamaa_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> sa propre chair.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्मन्</span> <i>ātman</i> [<a href="#entry-an_2" class="skt-link">an_2</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">*</a> r. on, se, soi (aux 3 pers.) — <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> souffle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> principe de vie; âme; esprit, intelligence; soi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> essence, caractère, nature; particularité; corps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] np. d'Ātmā, «le Soi» ou Âme universelle, essence immuable de l'Être, forme microcosmique du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'Âme, l'une des 9 substances [<a href="#entry-dravya_1" class="skt-link">dravya_1</a>] du <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a> — <a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>ātma</i> ifc. dont l'esprit est <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ατμος; all. Odem; fr. âme, atmosphère; pali <i>attā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्मना</span> <i>ātmanā</i> [i. <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] adv. par soi-même — iic. (avec n-ième ordinal) étant soi-même (le n-ième).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmanaadvitiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmanādvitīya</i> [<a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">dvitīya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmanaadvitiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmanaadvitiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmanaadvitiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmanādvitīyā</i> étant soi-même le second.<br> <a href="#entry-aatmanaa" class="skt-link">ātmanā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmane">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्मने</span> <i>ātmane</i> [dat. <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] iic. .
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmanepada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmanepada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmanepada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. [«mot pour soi»] voix réfléchie (moyen): l'action est pour son propre bénéfice; opp. <a href="#entry-parasmaipada" class="skt-link">parasmaipada</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmanepadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmanepadin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmanepadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmanepadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmanepadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmanepadinī</i> gram. (se dit d'un vb.) se conjuguant en <a href="#entry-aatmanepada" class="skt-link">ātmanepada</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aatmanepada" class="skt-link">ātmanepada</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aatmane" class="skt-link">ātmane</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmapak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmapakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmapak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> son propre parti.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaprabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaprabha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabha" class="skt-link">prabha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmaprabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaprabha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmaprabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmaprabhā</i> qui a sa propre splendeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaprasaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaprasāda</i> [<a href="#entry-prasaada" class="skt-link">prasāda</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmaprasaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. grâce divine du soi; sérénité de l'âme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Ātmapurāṇa, œuvre de <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraananda" class="skt-link">Śaṃkarānanda</a>; il donne une forme versifiée des principales leçons [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>]; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uupani_sadratna" class="skt-link">Upaniṣadratna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmasamarpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmasamarpaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-samarpa_na" class="skt-link">samarpaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmasamarpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. abandon du soi, reddition du soi (à Dieu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmasandeha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmasaṃdeha</i> [<a href="#entry-sandeha" class="skt-link">saṃdeha</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmasa_mdeha" class="skt-link">m.</a> danger personnel, risque pour soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmasiddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmasiddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmasiddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. réalisation du soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmasiddhizaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmasiddhiśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmasiddhizaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. science de la réalisation du soi.<br> <a href="#entry-aatmasiddhi" class="skt-link">ātmasiddhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmatattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmatattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmatattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. vraie nature de l'Esprit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmatattvavivekadiidhiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmatattvavivekadīdhiti</i> [<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>-<a href="#entry-diidhiti" class="skt-link">dīdhiti</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmatattvavivekadiidhiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Ātmatattvavivekadīdhiti «Réflexion sur le discernement de la vraie nature de l'Esprit», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha_1" class="skt-link">Raghunātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Uziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Śiromaṇi</a>; c'est un commentaire de la réfutation du bouddhisme par <a href="#entry-Uudayana_1" class="skt-link">Udayana_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aatmatattva" class="skt-link">ātmatattva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmatu_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmatuṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-tu_s_ti" class="skt-link">tuṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmatu_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [«ce qui plaît à soi-même»] ce qui est bon, moral ou beau en conscience.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmaupamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaupamya</i> [<a href="#entry-aupamya" class="skt-link">aupamya</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmaupamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> comparaison à soi-même; fait de se mettre à la place de qqn. d'autre.<br> <a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmavadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmavadha</i> [<a href="#entry-vadha" class="skt-link">vadha</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmavadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> suicide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmavatī</i> qui a une âme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réfléchi, réservé; prudent — adv. par soi-même, comme soi-même.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmavizraanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaviśrānti</i> [<a href="#entry-vizraanti" class="skt-link">viśrānti</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmavizraanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. le repos dans le Soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmazakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaśakti</i> [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmazakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> son propre pouvoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmazo_nita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmaśoṇita</i> [<a href="#entry-zo_nita" class="skt-link">śoṇita</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmazo_nita" class="skt-link">n.</a> son propre sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmecchaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmecchā</i> [<a href="#entry-icchaa" class="skt-link">icchā</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmecchaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> préférence personnelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्मीय</span> <i>ātmīya</i> [<a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatmiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aatmiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ātmīyā</i> son propre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatmopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ātmopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-aatmopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'Ātmopaniṣad.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatreya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्रेय</span> <i>ātreya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-atri" class="skt-link">atri</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-aatreya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du sage Ātreya, disciple de <a href="#entry-Ubharadvaaja" class="skt-link">Bharadvāja</a> qui lui appris la science médicale [<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>]; il eut 6 disciples, dont <a href="#entry-Uagniveza" class="skt-link">Agniveśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आत्त</span> <i>ātta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aadaa" class="skt-link">ādā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āttā</i> pris, enlevé; reçu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aatura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आतुर</span> <i>ātura</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-tura" class="skt-link">tura</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aatura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aatura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaturaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āturā</i> qui souffre; malade — ifc. affligé par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désireux de ⟨inf.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaturasannyaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āturasannyāsin</i> [<a href="#entry-sannyaasin" class="skt-link">sannyāsin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaturasannyaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaturasannyaasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaturasa_mnyaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āturasaṃnyāsinī</i> soc. renonçant prononçant ses vœux sur son lit de mort.<br> <a href="#entry-aatura" class="skt-link">ātura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aav_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवृ</span> <i>āvṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-v_r_1" class="skt-link">vṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(āvṛṇoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(āvṛṇute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āvṛta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i><i>)</i> couvrir, enfermer; protéger, cacher; enlever, retenir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aav_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवृष्टि</span> <i>āvṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-v_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">vṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-aav_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> pluie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aav_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवृत</span> <i>āvṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aav_r" class="skt-link">āvṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aav_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aav_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aav_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvṛtā</i> couvert de; vêtu de; plein de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entouré de; protégé; enclos, caché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aav_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवृत्त</span> <i>āvṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aav_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aav_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aav_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvṛttā</i> tourné; agité, enroulé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> contourné, évité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a battu retraite, enfui — <a href="#entry-aav_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'adresser une prière ou des louanges à un dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवृत्ति</span> <i>āvṛtti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> retour au point de départ; répétition, récurrence; occurrence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de tourner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> existence; ronde des réincarnations <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. multiplication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. (figure de style) répétition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaabhyaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाभ्याम्</span> <i>āvābhyām</i> [i. du. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. par nous deux — dat. pour nous deux — abl. hors de nous deux.
  </div>
</div>
