<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाह्</span> <i>āvāh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-aavah" class="skt-link">āvah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(āvāhayati)</i> inviter.<br> <i>gurum</i> <i>āvāhayāmi</i> <i>sthāpayāmi</i> <i>nāradam</i> <i>āvāhayāmi</i> <i>sthāpayāmi</i> (rituel de <a href="#entry-diik_saa" class="skt-link">dīkṣā</a>) J'invoque le maître et lui demande de présider, j'invoque <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a> et lui demande de présider.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाह</span> <i>āvāha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aavaah" class="skt-link">āvāh</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> invitation; marriage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाहन</span> <i>āvāhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aavaah" class="skt-link">āvāh</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaahana" class="skt-link">n.</a> invitation, appel; invocation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [tantr.] rite d'invocation d'une divinité dans une idole ou dans la personne d'un officiant — <a href="#entry-aavaahanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvāhanī</i> soc. posture des mains [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] d'invocation ou d'offrande; cf. <a href="#entry-aavaahanamudraa" class="skt-link">āvāhanamudrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaahanamudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āvāhanamudrā</i> var. <i>āvāhanī</i> [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaahanamudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. geste rituel de <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>, les deux mains en coupe pour faire l'offrande de fleurs à l'idole en invoquant la divinité, les pouces touchant la racine des auriculaires.<br> <a href="#entry-aavaahana" class="skt-link">āvāhana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाम्</span> <i>āvām</i> [nom. du. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. nous deux — acc. à nous deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवाप</span> <i>āvāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vaapa_2" class="skt-link">vāpa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de disperser; fait de semer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> insertion, arrangement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. ajout d'ingrédients à un remède.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवार</span> <i>āvāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aav_r" class="skt-link">āvṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> abri; défense, place forte — ifc. qui abrite, qui défend.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवास</span> <i>āvāsa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-aavas" class="skt-link">āvas</a>] <a href="#entry-aavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> demeure, résidence; maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवासिन्</span> <i>āvāsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aavas" class="skt-link">āvas</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aavaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavaasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvāsinī</i> qui habite; résident de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavadh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवध्</span> <i>āvadh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vadh" class="skt-link">vadh</a>] aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(āvadhīt)</i> détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवह्</span> <i>āvah</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āvahati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>auḍha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i><i>)</i> apporter auprès de; approcher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> donner, procurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> emmener chez soi (not. la mariée); porter — ca. <i>(āvāhayati)</i> inviter.<br> <i>vinodam</i> <i>āvah</i> procurer du plaisir.<br> <i>saukhyam</i> <i>āvah</i> procurer la félicité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवह</span> <i>āvaha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aavah" class="skt-link">āvah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvahā</i> qui produit, qui procure, qui apporte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui ramène à la maison (une épouse) — <a href="#entry-aavaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Āvaha, vent de l'une des 7 voies du ciel [<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>]; il souffle dans la région intermédiaire ou atmosphère [<a href="#entry-bhuvarloka" class="skt-link">bhuvarloka</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'une des 7 langues d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवलि</span> <i>āvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vali" class="skt-link">vali</a>] <a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> suite, ligne, rangée; dynastie — ifc. lit. recueil de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>āvalī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवन्त</span> <i>āvanta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-avanti" class="skt-link">avanti</a>] <a href="#entry-aavanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi d'<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a> — <a href="#entry-aavantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvantī</i> langue d'<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavantaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवन्तक</span> <i>āvantaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-avanti" class="skt-link">avanti</a><a href="#entry--aka" class="skt-link">-aka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavantaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavantaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaavantikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āavantikā</i> qui concerne les <a href="#entry-Uavantayas" class="skt-link">Avantayas</a> — <a href="#entry-aaavantikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āavantikā</i> manière de parler, style de composition [<a href="#entry-riiti" class="skt-link">rīti</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavantya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवन्त्य</span> <i>āvantya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-avanti" class="skt-link">avanti</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavantya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavantya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavantyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvantyā</i> originaire d'<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. myth. le peuple des Āvantyās, une tribu des <a href="#entry-Uhaihayaas" class="skt-link">Haihayās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavantyakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āvantyakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-aavantyakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavantyakha_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āvantyakhaṇḍa, section du <a href="#entry-Uskandapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Skandapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aavantya" class="skt-link">āvantya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवरण</span> <i>āvaraṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aav_r" class="skt-link">āvṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavara_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvaraṇā</i> couvrant, protégeant — <a href="#entry-aavara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> voile, couverture, protection; enclos, barrière; mur; bouclier, armure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. pouvoir d'illusion de la <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a> dissimulant la vraie nature divine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. motif circulaire d'un <a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>; il y en a 9 dans le <a href="#entry-zriiyantra" class="skt-link">śrīyantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवर्त</span> <i>āvarta</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aavarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> tourbillon, gouffre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> boucle de cheveux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> territoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavartaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवर्तक</span> <i>āvartaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavartaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartikā</i> qui tourbillonne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourneboulé — <a href="#entry-aavartaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> tourbillon; tornade; boucle de cheveux — <a href="#entry-aavartakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartakī</i> natu. bot. <i>Cassia auriculata</i>, arbuste buissonnant aux feuilles composées, aux fleurs jaune vif en corymbes, utilisé en tannerie et en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-carmarafgaa" class="skt-link">carmaraṅgā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavartamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवर्तमान</span> <i>āvartamāna</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavartamaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartamaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartamānā</i> qui tourne en rond; qui se vrille, qui se retourne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va de l'avant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavartana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवर्तन</span> <i>āvartana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aavartana" class="skt-link">n.</a> rotation; gyration; demi-tour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mise en rotation, barattage; agitation (not. métal en fusion) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> répétition; étude, récitation; pratique — <a href="#entry-aavartana" class="skt-link">m.</a> tour de magie — <a href="#entry-aavartanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartanī</i> creuset.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavartin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवर्तिन्</span> <i>āvartin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aav_rt_1" class="skt-link">āvṛt_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavartin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavartinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartinī</i> qui tourne sur soi-même, qui vrille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui retourne — <a href="#entry-aavartin" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheval au poil bouclé, un signe de chance — <a href="#entry-aavartinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvartinī</i> tornade; tourbillon; vortex.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवस्</span> <i>āvas</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āvasati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i><i>)</i> habiter, demeurer; prendre possession d'un logement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavasatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवसथ</span> <i>āvasatha</i> [<a href="#entry-aavas" class="skt-link">āvas</a><a href="#entry--tha" class="skt-link">-tha</a>] <a href="#entry-aavasatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> logement, habitation; hutte; abri pour la nuit; village.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavasathya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āvasathya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavasathya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavasathya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavasathyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvasathyā</i> résident — <a href="#entry-aavasathya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. feu domestique; c'est l'un des <a href="#entry-pa_ncaagni" class="skt-link">pañcāgni</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aavasatha" class="skt-link">āvasatha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavasati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवसति</span> <i>āvasati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aavas" class="skt-link">āvas</a>] <a href="#entry-aavasati" class="skt-link">f.</a> abri; logement pour la nuit; nuit de repos.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavayos">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवयोस्</span> <i>āvayos</i> [loc. du. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. en nous deux — g. de nous deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavazyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवश्यक</span> <i>āvaśyaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-avazya" class="skt-link">avaśya</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavazyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavazyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavazyikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvaśyikā</i> nécessaire, inévitable; irrépressible — <a href="#entry-aavazyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> nécessité, obligation; besoin naturel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. l'un des 6 actes rituels quotidiens obligatoires: la sérénité [<a href="#entry-saamaayika" class="skt-link">sāmāyika</a>], la vénération du supérieur de l'ordre [<a href="#entry-vandana" class="skt-link">vandana</a>], la confession [<a href="#entry-pratikrama_na" class="skt-link">pratikramaṇa</a>], le vœu d'ascétisme [<a href="#entry-pratyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">pratyākhyāna</a>], la renonciation au corps [<a href="#entry-kaayotsarga" class="skt-link">kāyotsarga</a>] et la vénération des icônes et des temples [<a href="#entry-caityavandana" class="skt-link">caityavandana</a>].<br> <i>āvaśyakam</i> <i>vā</i> avez vous besoin de qqc.?<br> <i>punar</i> <i>annam</i> <i>āvaśyakam</i> <i>vā</i> dois-je vous resservir?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavega">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवेग</span> <i>āvega</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vega" class="skt-link">vega</a>] <a href="#entry-aavega" class="skt-link">m.</a> hâte, agitation, excitation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaveza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवेश</span> <i>āveśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aaviz" class="skt-link">āviś</a>] <a href="#entry-aaveza" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'entrer, de prendre possession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de s'absorber complètement dans une tâche ou une idée; dévotion intense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possession par un esprit; épilepsie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrogance; orgueil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविष्ट</span> <i>āviṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aaviz" class="skt-link">āviś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āviṣṭā</i> pénétré, entré dans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possédé; épileptique — ifc. rempli de; sujet à; ayant pour tâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaviddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविद्ध</span> <i>āviddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aavyadh" class="skt-link">āvyadh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaviddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaviddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaviddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āviddhā</i> jeté; chassé, renvoyé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> percé, blessé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> balancé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excité; énervé — <a href="#entry-aaviddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> balancement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaviddhakar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āviddhakarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaviddhakar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaviddhakar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaviddhakar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āviddhakarṇī</i> qui a les oreilles percées.<br> <a href="#entry-aaviddha" class="skt-link">āviddha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविक</span> <i>āvika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-avi" class="skt-link">avi</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvikī</i> relatif au mouton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en laine — <a href="#entry-aavika" class="skt-link">n.</a> étoffe de laine — <a href="#entry-aavikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvikī</i> peau de mouton.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविल</span> <i>āvila</i> a. <a href="#entry-aavila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvilā</i> trouble (liquide, yeux), obscurci; souillé, not. de sang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>ābila</i> f. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> confus, embarrassé — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(āvilayati)</i> troubler, souiller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविर्</span> <i>āvir</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-aavis" class="skt-link">āvis</a>.<br> <i>āvirbhū</i> apparaître, devenir visible, se manifester.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavirbhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āvirbhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-aavirbhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> manifestation, apparition; présence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le pouvoir divin de manifestation; opp. <a href="#entry-tirobhaava" class="skt-link">tirobhāva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavirbhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āvirbhūta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aavirbhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aavirbhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aavirbhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āvirbhūtā</i> manifeste, visible.<br> <a href="#entry-aavir" class="skt-link">āvir</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविस्</span> <i>āvis</i> adv. ouvertement, manifestement; en vue.<br> <i>āvir</i> <i>as</i> apparaître, se manifester.<br> <i>āvir</i> <i>bhū</i> apparaître, se manifester.<br> <i>āviṣ</i> <i>kṛ</i> faire connaître, manifester; rendre visible; exhiber, montrer.<br> <i>āviṣkṛta</i> a. m. n. évident, connu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaviz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आविश्</span> <i>āviś</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-viz_1" class="skt-link">viś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(āviśate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āviṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> entrer dans, pénétrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavutta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आवुत्त</span> <i>āvutta</i> <a href="#entry-aavutta" class="skt-link">m.</a> beau-frère (mari d'une sœur); syn. <a href="#entry-bhaginiipati" class="skt-link">bhaginīpati</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (dérogatoire) proxénète, fournisseur du harem du roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aavyadh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आव्यध्</span> <i>āvyadh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-vyadh" class="skt-link">vyadh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(āvidhyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āviddha</i><i>)</i> chasser, expulser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> percer, blesser, frapper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> balancer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> agiter, exciter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आय</span> <i>āya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-e" class="skt-link">e</a>] <a href="#entry-aaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrivée; revenus, gains.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आया</span> <i>āyā</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yaa_1" class="skt-link">yā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(āyāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyāta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> arriver; en venir à tel état.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयाम</span> <i>āyāma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-aayam" class="skt-link">āyam</a>] <a href="#entry-aayaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> extension; étirement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> posture d'étirement; utilisée par les récitants véd. pour l'accent aigu [<a href="#entry-udaatta" class="skt-link">udātta</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étendue; longueur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयान</span> <i>āyāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aayaa" class="skt-link">āyā</a>] <a href="#entry-aayaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> arrivée; tempérament, disposition naturelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयास</span> <i>āyāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aayas" class="skt-link">āyas</a>] <a href="#entry-aayaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> effort, travail; fatigue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयम्</span> <i>āyam</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yam" class="skt-link">yam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āyacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> étendre, allonger — pr. md. <i>(āyacchate)</i> retenir, arrêter; contrôler, refréner, maîtriser — ca. <i>(āyamayati)</i> retenir, refréner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयम</span> <i>āyama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aayam" class="skt-link">āyam</a>] <a href="#entry-aayama" class="skt-link">m.</a> extension, longueur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> entrave; contrôle, maîtrise; maintien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयन</span> <i>āyana</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyanā</i> relatif au solstice — <a href="#entry-aayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyanī</i> ifc. descendant de (forme des patr.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयन्य</span> <i>āyanya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-aayanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils de (forme des patr.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयस्</span> <i>āyas</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yas" class="skt-link">yas</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(āyasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyasta</i>, <i>āyasita</i><i>)</i> s'efforcer, se fatiguer, s'épuiser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयस</span> <i>āyasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ayas" class="skt-link">ayas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyasī</i> de fer, en fer: métallique — <a href="#entry-aayasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> métal; fer — <a href="#entry-aayasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyasī</i> cuirasse (métallique), armure, cotte de maille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयसित</span> <i>āyasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayas" class="skt-link">āyas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyasitā</i> fatigué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयस्त</span> <i>āyasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayas" class="skt-link">āyas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyastā</i> effectué avec difficulté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui se fatigue; zélé; anxieux; fatigué, fourbu — <a href="#entry-aayasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> énergie; acte énergique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयत्</span> <i>āyat</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yat_1" class="skt-link">yat_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āyatate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyatta</i><i>)</i> arriver, atteindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être à la disposition de, dépendre de ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयत</span> <i>āyata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayam" class="skt-link">āyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyatā</i> étendu, long, grand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayatalocana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyatalocana</i> [<a href="#entry-locana" class="skt-link">locana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayatalocana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayatalocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayatalocanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyatalocanā</i> qui a de grands yeux.<br> <a href="#entry-aayata" class="skt-link">āyata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayatana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयतन</span> <i>āyatana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aayat" class="skt-link">āyat</a>] <a href="#entry-aayatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> demeure, résidence, patrie; siège, refuge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. sanctuaire, temple [«demeure de dieu»]; emplacement du foyer sacré, autel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. site de perception sensorielle ou de la pensée; cf. <a href="#entry-_sa_daayatana" class="skt-link">ṣaḍāyatana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayatanavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyatanavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-aayatanavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [ChU.] le Cognitif ou partition [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> en quatre cognitions: le souffle vital [<a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>], la vue [<a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>], l'ouïe [<a href="#entry-zrotra" class="skt-link">śrotra</a>] et le mental [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-aayatana" class="skt-link">āyatana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयत्त</span> <i>āyatta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayat" class="skt-link">āyat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayatta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyattā</i> dépendant; à disposition, prêt; prudent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयोधन</span> <i>āyodhana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aayudh" class="skt-link">āyudh</a>] <a href="#entry-aayodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> combat; champ de bataille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayodhyika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयोध्यिक</span> <i>āyodhyika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ayodhyaa" class="skt-link">ayodhyā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayodhyika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayodhyika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayodhyikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyodhyikā</i> né, demeurant, ou situé à <a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayogava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयोगव</span> <i>āyogava</i> var. <i>ayogava</i> <a href="#entry-aayogava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayogavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyogavī</i> soc. descendant d'un <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> et d'une femme <a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a>; charpentier; cf. <a href="#entry-sairandhra" class="skt-link">sairaṃdhra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayu_h_s_toma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyuḥṣṭoma</i> [<a href="#entry-stoma" class="skt-link">stoma</a>] <a href="#entry-aayu_h_s_toma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rite pour obtenir une longue vie, partie de la cérémonie <a href="#entry-abhiplava" class="skt-link">abhiplava</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">āyus</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayu_smat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुष्मत्</span> <i>āyuṣmat</i> [<a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">āyus</a><a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayu_smat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayu_smat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayu_smatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyuṣmatī</i> bien portant; doué de longue vie; âgé, vénérable — <a href="#entry-aayu_smat" class="skt-link">m.</a> adr. Votre Éminence, Sire, Vénérable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Āyuṣmat «Longévité», fils de <a href="#entry-Usa_mhraada" class="skt-link">Saṃhrāda</a>.<br> <i>bho</i> <i>āyuṣmān</i> <i>edhi</i> <i>devadatta</i> (en retour à un salut) longue vie à toi, <a href="#entry-Udevadatta" class="skt-link">Devadatta</a> (si <a href="#entry-Udevadatta" class="skt-link">Devadatta</a> est de bonne caste [<a href="#entry-trivar_na" class="skt-link">trivarṇa</a>], on doit accentuer et surallonger sa dernière syllabe).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayu_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुष्य</span> <i>āyuṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">āyus</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayu_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayu_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayu_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyuṣyā</i> qui donne longue vie, sain, vital — <a href="#entry-aayu_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> force vitale, longévité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुध्</span> <i>āyudh</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yudh_1" class="skt-link">yudh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(āyudhyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(āyudhyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyuddha</i><i>)</i> combattre; attaquer, s'opposer à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुध</span> <i>āyudha</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-aayudh" class="skt-link">āyudh</a>] <a href="#entry-aayudha" class="skt-link">n.</a> arme — ifc. armé de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudhaagaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyudhāgāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aagaara" class="skt-link">āgāra</a>] <a href="#entry-aayudhaagaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> armurerie, arsenal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudhaagaaranara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyudhāgāranara</i> [<a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">nara</a>] <a href="#entry-aayudhaagaaranara" class="skt-link">m.</a> gouverneur d'un arsenal.<br> <a href="#entry-aayudhaagaara" class="skt-link">āyudhāgāra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aayudha" class="skt-link">āyudha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudhapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyudhapuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-aayudhapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> arch. figuration anthropomorphe d'une arme d'un dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudhiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुधीय</span> <i>āyudhīya</i> [<a href="#entry-aayudha" class="skt-link">āyudha</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayudhiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayudhiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayudhiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyudhīyā</i> qui vit du métier des armes — <a href="#entry-aayudhiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> guerrier, soldat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayudhika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुधिक</span> <i>āyudhika</i> [<a href="#entry-aayudha" class="skt-link">āyudha</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayudhika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayudhika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayudhikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyudhikā</i> concernant les armes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui vit du métier des armes — <a href="#entry-aayudhika" class="skt-link">m.</a> guerrier, soldat — <a href="#entry-aayudhika" class="skt-link">n.</a> trafic d'armes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुज्</span> <i>āyuj</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-yuj_1" class="skt-link">yuj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(āyunakti)</i> pr. md. <i>(āyuṅkte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āyukta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> joindre, attacher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुक्त</span> <i>āyukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayuj" class="skt-link">āyuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyuktā</i> chargé de — <a href="#entry-aayukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> employé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayuktaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyuktaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-aayuktaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> personnage exerçant une charge, fonctionnaire.<br> <a href="#entry-aayukta" class="skt-link">āyukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुर्</span> <i>āyur</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">āyus</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayurdaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyurdā</i> [<a href="#entry-daa" class="skt-link">dā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayurdaa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayurdaa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayurdaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui donne la vie; qui donne longue vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayurveda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyurveda</i> [<a href="#entry-veda" class="skt-link">veda</a>] <a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">m.</a> médecine [«science de la force vitale»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. l'Āyurveda, traité de médecine traditionnelle, un <a href="#entry-upaveda" class="skt-link">upaveda</a>; il comprend 8 sections: <a href="#entry-zalya" class="skt-link">śalya</a> l'extraction des corps étrangers (épines, flèches), <a href="#entry-zaalaakya" class="skt-link">śālākya</a> la chirurgie de l'œil et de l'oreille, <a href="#entry-kaayacikitsaa" class="skt-link">kāyacikitsā</a> la médecine du corps, <a href="#entry-bhuutavidyaa" class="skt-link">bhūtavidyā</a> la psychiatrie ou science des esprits malins, <a href="#entry-kaumaarabh_rtya" class="skt-link">kaumārabhṛtya</a> la pédiatrie, <a href="#entry-agadatantra" class="skt-link">agadatantra</a> la science des antidotes, <a href="#entry-raasaayanatantra" class="skt-link">rāsāyanatantra</a> la science des élixirs, et <a href="#entry-vaajiikara_natantra" class="skt-link">vājīkaraṇatantra</a> la science des aphrodisiaques; cf. <a href="#entry-Udhanvantari" class="skt-link">Dhanvantari</a>, <a href="#entry-Usuzruta" class="skt-link">Suśruta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aayur" class="skt-link">āyur</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayuryoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āyuryoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-aayuryoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. conjonction de planètes utilisée en astrologie prédictive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुस्</span> <i>āyus</i> [<a href="#entry-aayu_1" class="skt-link">āyu_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">n.</a> âge, longévité; vie, santé; vigueur, force vitale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le vivant — <a href="#entry-aayus" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Āyu «le Vigoureux», 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ roi de la lignée lunaire, fils de <a href="#entry-Upuruuravaa" class="skt-link">Purūravā</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uurvazii" class="skt-link">Urvaśī</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Unahu_sa" class="skt-link">Nahuṣa</a>; il alla vers l'Est, et ses descendants peuplèrent la plaine Indo-Gangétique, tandis que son frère <a href="#entry-Uamaavasu" class="skt-link">Amāvasu</a> resta à l'Ouest <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. αιον; lat. <i>aevum</i>; fr. âge.<br> <i>āyurghṛtanyāya</i> phil. maxime de la longévité et du beurre, évoquant la relation entre la cause et l'effet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aayuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आयुत</span> <i>āyuta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aayu_2" class="skt-link">āyu_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aayuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aayuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aayutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āyutā</i> fondu; mélangé — ifc. combiné avec — <a href="#entry-aayuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> beurre fondu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaza_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशंस्</span> <i>āśaṃs</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-za_ms" class="skt-link">śaṃs</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āśaṃsati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśasta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>āśaṃsanīya</i><i>)</i> espérer, implorer; déclarer — ca. <i>(āśaṃsayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśaṃsita</i><i>)</i> déclarer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaza_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशंस</span> <i>āśaṃsa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aaza_ms" class="skt-link">āśaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaza_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṃsā</i> qui attend, qui espère — <a href="#entry-aaza_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṃsā</i> souhait, désir; espoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaza_msaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशंसनीय</span> <i>āśaṃsanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-aaza_ms" class="skt-link">āśaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaza_msaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṃsanīyā</i> désirable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaza_msita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशंसित</span> <i>āśaṃsita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-aaza_ms" class="skt-link">āśaṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaza_msita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaza_msitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṃsitā</i> espéré; déclaré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशास्</span> <i>āśās</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zaas" class="skt-link">śās</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(āśāste)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśiṣṭa</i>, <i>āśasta</i><i>)</i> désirer, espérer, prier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazaasti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशास्ति</span> <i>āśāsti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aazaas" class="skt-link">āśās</a>] <a href="#entry-aazaasti" class="skt-link">f.</a> prière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशावत्</span> <i>āśāvat</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaa_2" class="skt-link">āśā_2</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśāvatī</i> qui espère; qui garde l'espoir; qui a confiance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazafk">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशङ्क्</span> <i>āśaṅk</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zafk" class="skt-link">śaṅk</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āśaṅkate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśaṅkita</i><i>)</i> craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazafkaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशङ्का</span> <i>āśaṅkā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aazafk" class="skt-link">āśaṅk</a>] <a href="#entry-aazafkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> doute; incertitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inquiétude; suspicion, défiance — ifc. objection à — <a href="#entry-aazafka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>āśaṅka</i> ifc. crainte de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazafkaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशङ्कनीय</span> <i>āśaṅkanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-aazafk" class="skt-link">āśaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazafkaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazafkaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazafkaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṅkanīyā</i> suspect; contestable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazafkita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशङ्कित</span> <i>āśaṅkita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aazafk" class="skt-link">āśaṅk</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazafkita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazafkita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazafkitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśaṅkitā</i> craint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui inspire le doute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशस्</span> <i>āśas</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaas" class="skt-link">āśās</a>] <a href="#entry-aazas" class="skt-link">f.</a> vœu, désir, souhait; espoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazauca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशौच</span> <i>āśauca</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zauca" class="skt-link">śauca</a>] <a href="#entry-aazauca" class="skt-link">n.</a> impureté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशय</span> <i>āśaya</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-aazii" class="skt-link">āśī</a>] <a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit; lieu de repos, retraite, asile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> emplacement, localité; récipient, réceptacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cœur; esprit; intention <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] ensemble des mérites et démérites du <a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>; destin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. réceptacle, organe creux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazcarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्चर्य</span> <i>āścarya</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-carya" class="skt-link">carya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazcarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazcarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazcaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āścaryā</i> merveilleux, étonnant, extraordinaire — <a href="#entry-aazcarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> merveille, prodige — acc. <i>āścaryam</i> interj. ô merveille! — i. <i>āścaryeṇa</i> adv. par miracle.<br> <i>āścaryaṃ</i> <i>gavāṃ</i> <i>doho</i> <i>'gopena</i> [S.K.] La traite des vaches sans vacher est une merveille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazcaryavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āścaryavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazcaryavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazcaryavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazcaryavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āścaryavatī</i> prodigieux.<br> <a href="#entry-aazcarya" class="skt-link">āścarya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशी</span> <i>āśī</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zii_1" class="skt-link">śī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(āśete)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśayita</i><i>)</i> être étendu, reposer sur — ca. <i>(āśāyayati)</i> étendre sur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaziir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशीर्</span> <i>āśīr</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-aazis_1" class="skt-link">āśis_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaziirlif">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśīrliṅ</i> [<a href="#entry-lif" class="skt-link">liṅ</a>] nota. gram. <a href="#entry-lakaara" class="skt-link">lakāra</a> du précatif ou bénédictif [«optatif de souhait»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaziirvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśīrvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-aaziirvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> bénédiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaziirvaadaabhidhaanavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśīrvādābhidhānavat</i> [<a href="#entry-abhidhaana" class="skt-link">abhidhāna</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aaziirvaadaabhidhaanavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aaziirvaadaabhidhaanavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aaziirvaadaabhidhaanavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśīrvādābhidhānavatī</i> (nom) qui contient une formule de bénédiction.<br> <a href="#entry-aaziirvaada" class="skt-link">āśīrvāda</a> <br> <a href="#entry-aaziir" class="skt-link">āśīr</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aaziivi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśīviṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-vi_sa" class="skt-link">viṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-aaziivi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> serpent venimeux.<br> <a href="#entry-aazii_1" class="skt-link">āśī_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशिन्</span> <i>āśin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-az_2" class="skt-link">aś_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-aazin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśinī</i> qui mange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशिन</span> <i>āśina</i> [<a href="#entry-aaza_1" class="skt-link">āśa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśinā</i> véd. (qui a atteint un grand âge) âgé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशित</span> <i>āśita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aaz_2" class="skt-link">āś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśitā</i> nourri, repu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazle_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्लेष</span> <i>āśleṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aazli_s" class="skt-link">āśliṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-aazle_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> contact, mélange; embrassade, entrelacement, entremêlement — <a href="#entry-aazle_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśleṣā</i> pl. astr. np. des Āśleṣās «les Enlaçantes», ensemble de 5 étoiles formant le 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à la constellation de l'Hydre; les <a href="#entry-sarpaas" class="skt-link">sarpās</a> y président; son symbole est une roue de potier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazli_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्लिष्</span> <i>āśliṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zli_s" class="skt-link">śliṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(āśliṣyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśliṣṭa</i><i>)</i> embrasser; adhérer à ⟨loc. acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazli_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्लिष्ट</span> <i>āśliṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aazli_s" class="skt-link">āśliṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazli_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazli_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśliṣṭā</i> étreint, embrassé, serré; entrelacé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui adhère; qui étreint, qui embrasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रम</span> <i>āśrama</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zrama" class="skt-link">śrama</a>] <a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">n.</a> retraite, asile, refuge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ermitage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> stade de la vie brahmanique; cf. <a href="#entry-caturaazrama" class="skt-link">caturāśrama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazramadharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśramadharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-aazramadharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. règle de vie brahmanique, divisant l'existence en 4 étapes [<a href="#entry-caturaazrama" class="skt-link">caturāśrama</a>], chacune ayant ses devoirs propres [<a href="#entry-puru_saartha" class="skt-link">puruṣārtha</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazramapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśramapada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-aazramapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> syn. <a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">āśrama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazramavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśramavāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasa_1" class="skt-link">vāsa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-aazramavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> séjour en ermitage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazramavaasika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśramavāsika</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazramavaasika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazramavaasika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazramavaasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśramavāsikā</i> relatif au séjour à l'ermitage — <a href="#entry-aazramavaasika" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>āśramavāsikaparvan</i>] lit. 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «du séjour à l'ermitage».<br> <a href="#entry-aazramavaasa" class="skt-link">āśramavāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazramin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśramin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazramin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazramin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrami_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśramiṇī</i> soc. qui suit les stades de la vie brahmanique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ermite.<br> <a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">āśrama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रव</span> <i>āśrava</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-aazru" class="skt-link">āśru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazrava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazravaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśravā</i> qui écoute; qui suit les ordres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> promesse, engagement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रय</span> <i>āśraya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-aazri" class="skt-link">āśri</a>] <a href="#entry-aazraya" class="skt-link">m.</a> support; siège; refuge; protection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> autorité (dont on dépend) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pratique, habitude, dépendance, addiction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> contiguité, vicinité; connection, relation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. relation de dépendance; sujet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. ensemble des 5 organes des sens, plus l'intellect [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>]; ils sont les récipients des sensations et concepts [<a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">āśrita</a>] issus des attributs [<a href="#entry-aalambana" class="skt-link">ālambana</a>] des objets perçus — ifc. qui repose sur; qui dépend de, qui concerne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrayabhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśrayabhūta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazrayabhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrayabhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrayabhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśrayabhūtā</i> devenu un protecteur — <a href="#entry-aazrayabhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> protecteur.<br> <a href="#entry-aazraya" class="skt-link">āśraya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrayaparaav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśrayaparāvṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-paraav_rtti" class="skt-link">parāvṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-aazrayaparaav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. renaissance spirituelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrayapariv_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśrayaparivṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-pariv_rtti" class="skt-link">parivṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-aazrayapariv_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«retournement de la base»] changement de sexe (de femme en homme) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. élimination irréversible des souillures et de leurs causes (caractérise l'état de <a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रि</span> <i>āśri</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zri" class="skt-link">śri</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(āśrayate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(āśrayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśrita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> chercher refuge auprès de, demander la protection de, avoir recours à, reposer sur, se retirer dans ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'attacher à, dépendre de, en référer à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रित</span> <i>āśrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-aazri" class="skt-link">āśri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśritā</i> attaché à, qui a rejoint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a recours à, qui cherche refuge en <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sujet à, dépendant de; qui concerne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui habite, qui réside à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui suit, qui pratique, qui observe; qui utilise <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> occupé, habité — <a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> sujet, serviteur; adepte — <a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. objet perçu par les sens ou conçu par l'esprit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazritatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśritatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-aazritatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> dépendance.<br> <a href="#entry-aazrita" class="skt-link">āśrita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्रु</span> <i>āśru</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zru" class="skt-link">śru</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(āśṛṇoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(āśṛṇute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśruta</i><i>)</i> écouter; entendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accepter; promettre — ca. <i>(āśṛāvayati)</i> faire entendre; annoncer; s'adresser à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. proférer (not. lors d'un rite).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आशु</span> <i>āśu</i> a. <a href="#entry-aazu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazu" class="skt-link">f.</a> rapide — <a href="#entry-aazu" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. cheval — adv. vite; rapidement, directement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ωκυς; lat. <i>ocior</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazuga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśuga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazuga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazuga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazugaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśugā</i> qui se déplace rapidement; agile, rapide.<br> <a href="#entry-aazu" class="skt-link">āśu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazukaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśukārin</i> [<a href="#entry-kaarin" class="skt-link">kārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazukaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazukaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazukaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśukāriṇī</i> qui agit rapidement; actif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazukavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśukavi</i> [<a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>] <a href="#entry-aazukavi" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. poète expert d'improvisation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्व</span> <i>āśva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśvī</i> équestre; relatif au cheval; tiré par un cheval — <a href="#entry-aazva" class="skt-link">n.</a> gent chevaline; troupeau de chevaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvalaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्वलायन</span> <i>āśvalāyana</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azvala" class="skt-link">aśvala</a>-<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvalaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. d'Āśvalāyana «Descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uazvala" class="skt-link">Aśvala</a>», disciple présumé de <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka" class="skt-link">Śaunaka</a>, antérieur au 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.; on lui attribue les ouvrages de rituel qui suivent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvalaayanag_rhyasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśvalāyanagṛhyasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">gṛhyasūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvalaayanag_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āśvalāyanagṛhyasūtra, manuel de rituel domestique attribué à <a href="#entry-Uaazvalaayana" class="skt-link">Āśvalāyana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvalaayanazrautasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśvalāyanaśrautasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-zrautasuutra" class="skt-link">śrautasūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvalaayanazrautasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de l'Āśvalāyanaśrautasūtra, manuel de rituel solennel attribué à <a href="#entry-Uaazvalaayana" class="skt-link">Āśvalāyana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-aazvalaayana" class="skt-link">āśvalāyana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvamedhika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्वमेधिक</span> <i>āśvamedhika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazvamedhika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazvamedhika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazvamedhikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśvamedhikā</i> soc. qui concerne le sacrifice du cheval.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvamedhikaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>āśvamedhikaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvamedhikaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «du sacrifice du cheval».<br> <a href="#entry-aazvamedhika" class="skt-link">āśvamedhika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvapata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्वपत</span> <i>āśvapata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azvapati" class="skt-link">aśvapati</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvapata" class="skt-link">m.</a> patr. d'Āśvapata «descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uazvapati" class="skt-link">Aśvapati</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्वस्</span> <i>āśvas</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zvas_1" class="skt-link">śvas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(āśvasiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>āśvasita</i><i>)</i> reprendre son souffle; revivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avoir confiance; être tranquille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvayuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्वयुज</span> <i>āśvayuja</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azvayuj" class="skt-link">aśvayuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazvayuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazvayuja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazvayujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśvayujā</i> né sous le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Uazvayujau" class="skt-link">Aśvayujau</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif au mois <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a> — <a href="#entry-aazvayujii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśvayujī</i> astr. sa pleine lune; soc. on y pratique un <a href="#entry-paakayaj_na" class="skt-link">pākayajña</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazvina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्विन</span> <i>āśvina</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-azvin" class="skt-link">aśvin</a>] <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">m.</a> nom du mois d'<a href="#entry-Uazvinii" class="skt-link">Aśvinī</a>, à la saison des pluies (septembre-octobre); cf. <a href="#entry-azvatthaa" class="skt-link">aśvatthā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aazyaamala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आश्यामल</span> <i>āśyāmala</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-zyaamala" class="skt-link">śyāmala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-aazyaamala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-aazyaamala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-aazyaamalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>āśyāmalā</i> sombre, foncé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आञ्च्</span> <i>āñc</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-a_nc" class="skt-link">añc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ācati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākta</i><i>)</i> courber.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_nj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आञ्ज्</span> <i>āñj</i> [<a href="#entry-aa" class="skt-link">ā</a>-<a href="#entry-a_nj" class="skt-link">añj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ānakti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ākta</i><i>)</i> oindre — pr. md. <i>(āṅkte)</i> s'oindre de ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आञ्जन</span> <i>āñjana</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-a_njana" class="skt-link">añjana</a>] <a href="#entry-aa_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> onguent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>unguen</i>; fr. onguent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-aa_njaneya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आञ्जनेय</span> <i>āñjaneya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-a_njanaa" class="skt-link">añjanā</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-aa_njaneya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'Āñjaneya, matr. d'<a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> «fils d'<a href="#entry-Ua_njanaa" class="skt-link">Añjanā</a>».
  </div>
</div>
